    person_map = {person["person_id']: person for person in people}

# 原有修复链（与第一版 solve_instance 一致）
repair_people = [person_map[pid] for pid in sorted(unscheduled)]
repaired, _, _, _ = add_temporary_with_copt(repair_people, sorties,
person_map, dist, time_limit_s)
unscheduled.difference_update(repaired)
singleton_repaired = _repair_with_singleton_sorties(
    [person_map[pid] for pid in sorted(unscheduled)], sorties, person_map,
fleet, dist)
unscheduled.difference_update(singleton_repaired)
second_repair, _, _, _ = add_temporary_with_copt(
    [person_map[pid] for pid in sorted(unscheduled)], sorties, person_map,
dist, time_limit_s)
unscheduled.difference_update(second_repair)
retimed_repair = _retime_insert_mandatory(
    [person_map[pid] for pid in sorted(unscheduled)], sorties, person_map,
dist)
unscheduled.difference_update(retimed_repair)

# 新增：重定时-局部改线联合修复 MILP（替代贪心 _reroute_insert_mandatory）
repair_report = None
if unscheduled:
    repaired_milp, repair_report = run_repair(
    [person_map[pid] for pid in sorted(unscheduled)], sorties,
person_map, dist, time_limit=120.0)
    unscheduled.difference_update(repaired_milp)

if unscheduled:
    # 回退：贪心改线（MILP 邻域外）

    [person_map[pid] for pid in sorted(unscheduled)], sorties,
person_map, dist)
    unscheduled.difference_update(rerouted_repair)

# 新增：整日连续重定时抛光（保持路线与顺序）
sorties, retime_report = retime_day(sorties, person_map, dist)

## A.2.9 pipeline\_v3.py（358行）——V3 正式入口（顺序扩池+rc-ALNS 定价+字典序 COPT）

```python
pipeline_v3.py - V3 入口（对应 codex 审查后的 P0/P1 优化）
Q1/Q2:
A. 安全候选池 (build_route_pool, 不动 v2 口径)
B. 真实停靠顺序枚举扩池 (_make_candidate_preserve_order, 不再规范化)
C. 真实顺序 rc-ALNS 定价循环 (alns_v3.PricingALNSv3)
D. COPT 字典序多目标集合划分（总机时 → 油耗 → 在途时间 → 架次 → 座位利用率）
E. 独立消融：base4914 / +v2顺序 / +v3顺序 / +v3 rc定价 × (LP, gap=0.001, gap=0)
Q3:
沿用 v2 联合修复 MILP + 整日重定时 LP（本入口保证 V3 结果目录可复现）;
Stage-B 局部实验由 q3_stage_b_experiment.py 单独执行。
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import csv
import json
import pickle
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from common import load_distances, load_people
from pipeline_support import build_route_pool, fill_unassigned,
_passenger_stops, \
    _make_candidate_preserve_order
```

```python
from pipeline import write_q1q2
from pipeline_v2 import expand_pool_by_order, solve_q3_v2

def _sig(c):
    return (c["airport"], c["aircraft_type"], tuple(c["stops"]), tuple(sorted(c["pids"])))

def expand_pool_by_order_v3(cands, people, dist, ftsg, max_extra=30000, min_stops=3):
    """真实顺序枚举扩池：每个合法排列用 preserve-order 构造器评估。"""
    from alns_v2 import _legal_orders
    person_map = {p["person_id']: p for p in people}
    seen = {_sig(c) for c in cands}
    extra = []
    for col in cands:
    if len(extra) >= max_extra:
    break
    if len(col["stops"]) < min_stops:
    continue
    pids = list(col["pids"])
    base_stops = list(col["stops"])
    if len(base_stops) <= 4:
    orders = list(_legal_orders(base_stops, pids, person_map))
    else:
    # 5 停靠：原始顺序 + 单点 best-insert 邻域（避免 120 全排列爆炸）
    orders = [base_stops]
    for i in range(len(base_stops)):
    for j in range(len(base_stops) + 1):
    if i == j or i + 1 == j:
    continue
    trial = base_stops[:i] + base_stops[i + 1:]
    trial.insert(j - 1 if j > i else j, base_stops[i])
    orders.append(trial)
    for stops in orders:
    if len(extra) >= max_extra:
    break
    candidate = _make_candidate_preserve_order(
    col["airport"], pids, stops, person_map, dist, ftsg=ftsg)
    if candidate is None:
    continue
    signature = _sig(candidate)
    if signature in seen:
    continue
    seen.add(signature)
    extra.append(candidate)
    return extra

def run_pricing_rounds(cands, people, dist, ftsg, all_pids, rounds=3, seconds=60.0):
    """V3 真实顺序 rc-ALNS 定价。返回（新增列表，每轮记录）。”"""
    from copt_solver import solve_set_partitioning_lp
    from alns_v3 import PricingALNSv3

    seen = {_sig(c) for c in cands}
    added = []
    records = []
    for round_id in range(rounds):
```

```python
lp_obj, duals, lp_status, lp_elapsed = solve_set_partitioning_lp(
    cands, all_pids, dist, time_limit=60)
records.append({"round": round_id, "lp_obj": lp_obj, "lp_status": lp_status,
    "lp_elapsed": lp_elapsed, "n_new": 0, "alns_elapsed": None,
    "min_rc": None})
if lp_status != 1 or not duals:
    break
    alns = PricingALNSv3(people, dist, duals, ftsg=ftsg, seed=42 + round_id, max_iter=6000, time_limit_s=seconds, collect_columns=True, column_collect_interval=25)
started = time.time()
_, _ = alns.solve()
records[-1]["alns_elapsed"] = time.time() - started
new_columns = []
min_rc = 0.0
for col in alns.candidate_columns():
    rc = col["duration"] - sum(duals.get(pid, 0.0) for pid in col["pids"])
    min_rc = min(min_rc, rc)
    signature = _sig(col)
    if signature in seen:
    continue
    seen.add(signature)
    new_columns.append(col)
records[-1]["n_new"] = len(new_columns)
records[-1]["min_rc"] = min_rc
print(f" [rc-ALNS v3] round {round_id}: LP={lp_obj:.4f} 新增{len(new_columns)} 列 "
    f"min_rc={min_rc:.4f} ({records[-1]['alns_elapsed']:.1f}s)", flush=True)
    added.extend(new_columns)
    if not new_columns:
    break
    cands = cands + new_columns
return added, records

def _mip_and_lp(cands, all_pids, dist, person_map=None):
    from copt_solver import solve_set_partitioning, solve_set_partitioning_lp
    lp_obj, _, lp_status, lp_elapsed = solve_set_partitioning_lp(cands, all_pids, dist)
_, obj001, status001, elapsed001 = solve_set_partitioning(
    cands, all_pids, dist, time_limit=180, rel_gap=0.001)
_, obj000, status000, elapsed000 = solve_set_partitioning(
    cands, all_pids, dist, time_limit=180, rel_gap=0.0)
return {
    "columns": len(cands), "lp_obj": lp_obj, "lp_status": lp_status,
    "lp_elapsed": lp_elapsed,
    "mip_gap001_obj": obj001, "mip_gap001_status": status001,
    "mip_gap001_elapsed": elapsed001,
    "mip_gap000_obj": obj000, "mip_gap000_status": status000,
    "mip_gap000_elapsed": elapsed000,
}

def build_cached_pools(q, cache_dir):
    """缓存 base 池（v2 口径）以便多组消融复用。"""
    cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

ftsg = __import__( "ftsg").build_ftsg()
started = time.time()
base = build_route_pool(people, dist, rounds=8, use_ftsg=True)
build_elapsed = time.time() - started
extra_v2 = expand_pool_by_order(base, people, dist, ftsg)
extra_v3 = expand_pool_by_order_v3(base, people, dist, ftsg)
with open(cache, "wb") as f:

    "build_elapsed": build_elapsed}, f)
print(f" [pool cache] base={len(base)} +v2order={len(extra_v2)}
+v3order={len(extra_v3)} "
    f"( {build_elapsed:.1f}s)", flush=True)
return {"base": base, "extra_v2": extra_v2, "extra_v3": extra_v3,
    "build_elapsed": build_elapsed}

def run_ablation(q, cache_dir):
    _, dist = load_distances()
    people = load_people(q)
    all_pids = [p["person_id"] for p in people]
    ftsg = __import__( "ftsg").build_ftsg()
    pools = build_cached_pools(q, cache_dir)

    groups = []
    base = pools["base"]
    groups.append("A_base4914", base))
    groups.append("B_plus_v2_order", base + pools["extra_v2"])
    groups.append("C_plus_v3_order", base + pools["extra_v3"])
    pricing_added, pricing_records = run_pricing_rounds(
    base + pools["extra_v3"], people, dist, ftsg, all_pids, rounds=3,
seconds=45.0)
    groups.append("D_plus_v3_rc_alns", base + pools["extra_v3"] +
    pricing_added))

    rows = []
    for name, cands in groups:
    print(f"[ablation Q{q}] {name}: {len(cands)} columns", flush=True)
    rec = _mip_and_lp(cands, all_pids, dist)
    rec.update({ "q": q, "group": name})
    rows.append(rec)
    print(f" LP={rec['lp_obj']} gap001={rec['mip_gap001_obj']} "
    f"gap000={rec['mip_gap000_obj']}", flush=True)

    out_csv = cache_dir / f"q{q}_ablation.csv"
    fieldnames = ["q", "group", "columns", "lp_obj", "lp_status", "lp_elapsed",
    "mip_gap001_obj", "mip_gap001_status", "mip_gap001_elapsed",
    "mip_gap000_obj", "mip_gap000_status", "mip_gap000_elapsed"]
    with open(out_csv, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=fieldnames, extrasaction="ignore")
    writer.writeheader()
    writer.writerows(rows)
    with open(cache_dir / f"q{q}_pricing_records.json", "w", encoding="utf-8")
as f:
    json.dump(pricing_records, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
```

```python
print(f"saved {out_csv}")
return rows

def solve_q1q2_v3(q, dist, people, pricing_rounds=3, pricing_seconds=60.0,
    time_limit_s=180.0, pools_cache_dir=None):
    from copt_solver import solve_set_partitioning_lex
    from ftsg import build_ftsg

    ftsg = build_ftsg()
    base = None
    extra_v3 = None
    if pools_cache_dir is not None:
    cache = Path(pools_cache_dir) / f"q{q}_pools.pkl"
    if cache.exists():
    with open(cache, "rb") as f:
    cached = pickle.load(f)
    base, extra_v3 = cached["base"], cached["extra_v3"]
    print(f" [缓存池] base={len(base)} +v3order={len(extra_v3)}",
flush=True)
    if base is None:
    started = time.time()
    base = build_route_pool(people, dist, rounds=8, use_ftsg=True)
    print(f" [安全候选池] {len(base)} columns ({time.time()-started:.1f}s)", flush=True)
    started = time.time()
    extra_v3 = expand_pool_by_order_v3(base, people, dist, ftsg)
    print(f" [真实顺序扩池] +{len(extra_v3)} -> {len(base)+len(extra_v3)} "
    f"{time.time()-started:.1f}s)", flush=True)
    cands = base + extra_v3

    all_pids = [p["person_id"] for p in people]
    added, pricing_records = run_pricing_rounds(
    cands, people, dist, ftsg, all_pids, rounds=pricing_rounds,
    seconds=pricing_seconds)
    cands = cands + added

    person_map = {p["person_id']: p for p in people}
    selected, lex_report = solve_set_partitioning_lex(
    cands, all_pids, dist, person_map, time_limit=time_limit_s)
    print(f" [字典序 COPT] selected={len(selected)}/{len(cands)} "
    f"duration={lex_report.get('duration')} fuel={lex_report.get('fuel')}
    "f"travel={lex_report.get('travel')}
    utilization={lex_report.get('utilization')},
    flush=True)
    if not selected:
    from copt_solver import solve_set_partitioning
    selected, obj, status, _ = solve_set_partitioning(
    cands, all_pids, dist, time_limit=time_limit_s, rel_gap=0.0)
    lex_report = {"fallback_status": status, "primary_obj": obj}
    return selected, {
    "n_base": len(base), "n_extra_v3": len(extra_v3), "n_pricing": len(added),
    "pricing_records": pricing_records, "lex_report": lex_report,
    "pool_size": len(cands),
    }

def main():
```

## A.2.10 pipeline\_v4.py（231行）——V4 正式入口（V3 机制链+分数需求定向完整顺序扩池，Q1/Q2）

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
""V4 正式管线：分数需求定向完整顺序列生成 (Q1/Q2)。

在 V3（安全池 + 真实顺序扩池 + rc-ALNS 定价 + 字典序 COPT）基础上，
新增第四机制——分数列定向扩池：

V3 池内整数解已证明最优 (gap=0) 时，LP 松弛的分数结构即为改进的唯一线索。
V4 对 LP 分数列 (<=5 停靠点) 枚举全部合法停靠顺序（突破 V3 对 5 停靠
列仅做单点邻域、回避全排列的盲区），把新列注入候选池后重解字典序模型。

机制流程 (Q1/Q2 通用):
    1. 安全候选池（贪心插入启发式） — base
    2. 真实停靠顺序扩池 — extra_v3 (V3 机制)
    3. 分数列完整顺序定向扩池 — extra_v4 (V4 新增)
    4. 字典序五阶段集合划分 (COPT) — 首要目标+燃油+在途+利用率
    5. 严格校验 + 五项指标

用法:
    python pipeline_v4.py --q 2 --out results/experiments/v4-final-q2
    python pipeline_v4.py --q 1 --out results/experiments/v4-final-q1

import argparse
import csv
import json
import pickle
import time
from pathlib import Path

from common import load_distances, load_people
from pipeline_support import _make_candidate_preserve_order, fill_unassigned
from pipeline import write_q1q2
import coptpy as cp

def _sig(c):
    return (c["airport"], c["aircraft_type"], tuple(c["stops"]), tuple(sorted(c["pids"])))

def fractional_order_expansion(cands, all_pids, person_map, dist,
    max_frac_stops=5):
    """V4 核心机制：LP 分数列的完整合法顺序枚举扩池。

求解候选池上的 LP 松弛，对每个取值属于 (0,1) 的列（停靠点不超过
max_frac_stops)，枚举其全部合法停靠顺序并构造新列（保留乘客原始
指派集合），去重后返回新增列。
"""
```

```python
from alns_v2 import _legal_orders

env = cp.Envr()
model = env.createModel("v4-lp")
model.setParam(cp.COPT.Param-currenting, 0)
x = {j: model.addVar(lb=0.0, ub=1.0, obj=c["duration"])
    for j, c in enumerate(cands)}
for pid in all_pids:
    cols = [j for j, c in enumerate(cands) if pid in c["pids"]
    model.addConstr(cp.quicksum(x[j] for j in cols) == 1)
model.setObjective(cp.quicksum(c["duration"] * x[j] for j, c in enumerate(cands)),
    sense=cp.COPT.MINIMIZE)
model.solve()
lp_obj = model.objval
frac_idx = [j for j in range(len(cands)) if 1e-6 < x[j].x < 1 - 1e-6]

seen = {_sig(c) for c in cands}
added = []
for j in frac_idx:
    col = cands[j]
    pids = list(col["pids"])
    stops = list(col["stops"])
    if len(stops) > max_frac_stops:
    continue
    for s in _legal_orders(stops, pids, person_map):
    c = _make_candidate_preserve_order(col["airport"], pids, s,
    person_map, dist)
    if c is None:
    continue
    sg = _sig(c)
    if sg in seen:
    continue
    seen.add(sg)
    added.append(c)
return added, lp_obj, len(frac_idx)

def solve_q1q2_v4(q, dist, people, pools_cache_dir=None, time_limit_s=300.0,
    frac_rounds=1, pricing_rounds=3, pricing_seconds=60.0):
    from copt_solver import solve_set_partitioning_lex
    from ftsg import build_ftsg
    from pipeline_v3 import run_pricing_rounds

ftsg = build_ftsg()
base = extra_v3 = None
if pools_cache_dir is not None:
    cache = Path(pools_cache_dir) / f"q{q}_pools.pkl"
    if cache.exists():
    with open(cache, "rb") as f:
    cached = pickle.load(f)
    base, extra_v3 = cached["base"], cached["extra_v3"]
    print(f" [缓存池] base={len(base)} + v3order={len(extra_v3)", flush=True)
if base is None:
    from alns_v3 import build_route_pool, expand_pool_by_order_v3
    started = time.time()
    base = build_route_pool(people, dist, rounds=8, use_ftsg=True)
    print(f" [安全候选池] {len(base)} columns ({time.time()-started:.lf}s)", flush=True)
```

```python
started = time.time()
extra_v3 = expand_pool_by_order_v3(base, people, dist, ftsg)
print(f" [真实顺序扩池] + {len(extra_v3)} -> {len(base)+len(extra_v3)} "
    f"({time.time()-started:.1f}s)", flush=True)

cands = base + extra_v3
all_pids = [p["person_id"] for p in people]
person_map = {p["person_id']: p for p in people}

# V3 机制：rc-ALNS 真实顺序定价
if pricing_rounds > 0:
    added_pricing, pricing_records = run_pricing_rounds(
    cands, people, dist, ftsg, all_pids, rounds=pricing_rounds,
    seconds=pricing_seconds)
    cands = cands + added_pricing
    print(f" [rc-ALNS 定价] + {len(added_pricing)} -> {len(cands)}", flush=True)
else:
    pricing_records = []

frac_records = []
for r in range(frac_rounds):
    started = time.time()
    added, lp_obj, n_frac = fractional_order_expansion(
    cands, all_pids, person_map, dist)
    frac_records.append({
    "round": r, "lp_obj": lp_obj, "n_frac": n_frac,
    "added": len(added), "pool": len(cands) + len(added),
    "elapsed": round(time.time() - started, 2),
    })
    print(f" [V4 分数列扩池] 轮{r}: LP={lp_obj:.2f} 分数列={n_frac}" 
    f"新列={len(added)} -> 池 {len(cands)+len(added)} "
    f"({time.time()-started:.1f}s)", flush=True)
    if not added:
    break
    cands = cands + added

# 最终池 LP 松驰界（V4 池的收紧下界，用于论文的松弛差距分析）
env = cp.Envr()
lp_model = env.createModel("v4-final-lp")
lp_model.setParam(cp.COPT.Parampowering, 0)
xv = {j: lp_model.addVar(lb=0.0, ub=1.0, obj=c["duration"])
    for j, c in enumerate(cands)}
for pid in all_pids:
    cols = [j for j, c in enumerate(cands) if pid in c["pids"])
    lp_model.addConstr(cp.quicksum(xv[j] for j in cols) == 1)
lp_model.setObjective(
    cp.quicksum(c["duration"] * xv[j] for j, c in enumerate(cands)),
    sense=cp.COPT.MINIMIZE)
lp_model.solve()
lp_obj_final = lp_model.objval
print(f" [V4 最终池 LP 松驰] {lp_obj_final:.2f} ({len(cands)} 列)", flush=True)

started = time.time()
selected, lex_report = solve_set_partitioning_lex(
    cands, all_pids, dist, person_map, time_limit=time_limit_s)
print(f" [字典序 COPT] selected={len(selected)}/{len(cands)} "
    f"duration={lex_report.get('duration')} fuel={lex_report.get('fuel')}
"
```

```python
f"travel={lex_report.get('travel')} "
f"utilization={lex_report.get('utilization')} "
f"{time.time()-started:.1f}s)", flush=True)
return selected, {
    "n_base": len(base), "n_extra_v3": len(extra_v3),
    "n_pricing": len(cands) - len(base) - len(extra_v3)
    - sum(r["added"] for r in frac_records),
    "n_frac_expand": sum(r["added"] for r in frac_records),
    "pricing_records": pricing_records,
    "frac_expansion_records": frac_records,
    "lp_obj_final": lp_obj_final,
    "lex_report": {k: v for k, v in lex_report.items() if k != "stages"},
    "pool_size": len(cands),
}

def main():
    parser = argparseArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--q", type=int, default=2, choices=[1, 2])
    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=int, default=600)
    parser.add_argument("--frac-rounds", type=int, default=1)
    parser.add_argument("--pricing-rounds", type=int, default=3)
    parser.add_argument("--pricing-seconds", type=float, default=60.0)
    parser.add_argument("--pools-cache-dir", type=Path, default=None)
    parser.add_argument "--out", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.q not in (1, 2):
    raise SystemExit("pipeline_v4 仅支持 Q1/Q2 (Q3 维持 V3 结果)")

    out_dir = args.out
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    _, dist = load_distances()
    people = load_people(args.q)
    qtag = f"q{args.q}"
    print(f"[v4] Q{args.q}: 安全池 → 真实顺序扩池 → 分数列完整顺序扩池 → 字典序 COPT")
    t0 = time.time()
    selected, report = solve_q1q2_v4(
    args.q, dist, people, pools_cache_dir=args.pools_cache_dir,
    time_limit_s=args.timeout, frac_rounds=args.frac_rounds,
    pricing_rounds=args.pricing_rounds,
    pricing_seconds=args.pricing_seconds)
    sorties, unassigned = fill_unassigned(selected, people, dist)
    write_q1q2(sorties, people, out_dir, qname=qtag)
    report["total_elapsed_s"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)
    with open(out_dir / "v4_report.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(report, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)

    from strict_validator import validate_q12
    strict = validate_q12(out_dir / f"{qtag}-routes.csv",
    out_dir / f"{qtag}-assignments.csv", args.q)
    print(f"[严格校验] {qtag}: {'PASS' if strict['ok'] else 'FAIL'} "
    f"errors={len(strict['errors'])}")
    for err in strict["errors"][:10]:
    print(" ", err)
    from metrics_q12 import q12_metrics
    metrics = q12_metrics(out_dir / f"{qtag}-routes.csv",
    out_dir / f"{qtag}-assignments.csv", args.q)
    print(f"[指标] {qtag}: {metrics}")

## A.2.11 copt\_solver.py（514行）——COPT 字典序多目标集合划分求解封装

```python
copt_solver.py - COPT 8.0.6 集成模块 (方案 §三 5 个集成点)

集成点 1: Q1/Q2 集合划分 MILP (Set Partitioning)
集成点 2: Q3 阶段一 24 机队 Multi-Day 时空指派 MILP
集成点 3: Q3 阶段二 临时任务精确临态背包 MIP
集成点 4/5: 小规模精确下界 Benchmark + 多求解器对比

△ COPT 8 API 实测 (copt-solver skill):
- COPT.OPTIMAL == 1, COPT.INFEASIBLE == 2, COPT.TIMEOUT == 8
- 无 getStatus() / relgap / INTEGER_OPTIMAL; 用 model.status / model.objval
- INFEASIBLE 时读 x.x 报 CoptError → 先查 status 再读解

from __future__ import annotations

import time

from common import AIRCRAFT, flight_time, sortie_duration

# =================== 集成点 1: 集合划分

def solve_set_partitioning(candidate_sorties, all_pids, dist=None,
time_limit=180.0, rel_gap=0.001):
    """从候选架次池选最小总使用时间子集覆盖全部需求 (方案 §三(一))。

    候选架次：[{airport, stops, passengers: {fac: [pids]}, aircraft_type, refuel,
duration}]
    返回 (selected, obj, status, elapsed_s)。

    """
    import coptpy as cp

    all_pid_set = set(all_pids)
    valid_sorties = []
    candidate_pid_sets = []
    durations = []
    for s in candidate_sorties:
    pids = list(s.get("pids") or [pid for values in s.get("passengers",
    }).values() for pid in values])
    pid_set = set(pids)
    if not pid_set or len(pid_set) != len(pids) or not
    pid_set.issubset(all_pid_set):
    continue
    if len(s.get("stops", [])) > 5 or s.get("aircraft_type") not in AIRCRAFT:
```

```python
continue
    if s.get("duration") is None:
    if dist is None:
    from common import load_distances
    _, dist = load_distances()
    duration = sortie_duration(
    [s["airport"]] + s["stops"] + [s["airport"]],
    AIRCRAFT[s["aircraft_type"]], dist, s.get("refuel", {}))
    else:
    duration = s["duration"]
    valid_sorties.append(s)
    candidate_pid_sets.append(pid_set)
    durations.append(duration)

    covering_by_pid = {pid: [] for pid in all_pids}
    for route_id, pid_set in enumerate(candidate_pid_sets):
    for pid in pid_set:
    covering_by_pid[pid].append(route_id)
    missing = [pid for pid, route_ids in covering_by_pid.items() if not route_ids]
    if missing:
    return [], None, cp.COPT.INFEASIBLE, 0.0

    env = cp.Envr()
    model = env.createModel("Set_Partitioning")
    x = {}
    for r_id in range(len(valid_sorties)):
    x[r_id] = model.addVar(vtype=cp.COPT.BINARY, obj=duerations[r_id], name=f"x_{r_id}")

    # 每人覆盖约束 =]
    for pid in all_pids:
    model.addConstr(cp.quicksum(x[r_id] for r_id in covering_by_pid[pid]) == 1, name=f"cover_{pid}")

    model.setObjective(cp.quicksum(duration[r_id] * x[r_id] for r_id in x), sense=cp.COPT.MINIMIZE)
    model.setParam(cp.COPT.Param.TimeLimit, time_limit)
    model.setParam(cp.COPT.Param.RelGap, rel_gap)
    t0 = time.time()
    model.solve()
    elapsed = time.time() - t0
    status = model.status
    obj = model.objval if status == cp.COPT.OPTIMAL else None
    selected = []
    if status == cp.COPT.OPTIMAL:
    selected = [valid_sorties[r_id] for r_id in x if x[r_id].x > 0.5]
    return selected, obj, status, elapsed

# ---- 集成点 1b：字典
序多目标集合划分
def _column_route_metrics(sortie, dist, person_map):
    """单列五项线性/分式指标系数（用于字典序二级优化）。

    返回 duration / fuel / travel / n_sortie(=1) / seat_km / pax_km。
    在途时间按「停靠到达时刻 - 上机出发时刻」计算，与 metrics_q12 口径一致。
    """
    from pipeline_support import _passenger_intervals
    pid = list(sortie.get("pids") or [
    pid for values in sortie.get("passengers", {}).values() for pid in values]
    route = [sortie["airport"]] + sortie["stops"] + [sortie["airport"]]
    ac = AIRCRAFT[sortie["aircraft_type"]]
    duration = sortie_duration(route, ac, dist, sortie.get("refuel", {}))
    fuel = sum(dist[(i, j)] * ac.fuel_rate for i, j in zip(route, route[1:]))
    intervals = _passenger_intervals(route, pid, person_map)
    if intervals is None:
    intervals = []
    times = [0.0]
    current = 0.0
    for index in range(len(route) - 1):
    current += flight_time(dist[(route[index], route[index + 1])], ac)
    if index + 1 < len(route) - 1:
    current += 20 if sortie.get("refuel", {}).get(index + 1) else 10 times.append(current)
    travel = sum(times[delivery] - times[pickup] for _, pickup, delivery in intervals)
    loads = [0] * (len(route) - 1)
    for _, pickup, delivery in intervals:
    for segment in range(pickup, delivery):
    loads[segment] += 1
    seat_km = sum(dist[(route[i], route[i + 1])] * ac.seats for i in range(len(route) - 1))
    pax_km = sum(dist[(route[i], route[i + 1])] * loads[i] for i in range(len(route) - 1))
    return {
    "duration": duration,
    "fuel": fuel,
    "travel": travel,
    "n_sortie": 1,
    "seat_km": seat_km,
    "pax_km": pax_km,
    }

    started = time.time()

# =================== 集成点 2: Q3 机队指派
def solve_aircraft_assignment(aircraft_list, required_sorties, day, time_limit=300.0):
    """24 机 × 单日候选架次指派 (方案 §三(二))。

    aircraft_list: {{id, home_airport, type}]
    required_sorties: {{airport, aircraft_type, duration, depart_min, ...}]
    返回 (assigned: {sortie_idx: aircraft_id}, obj, status)。

    """
    import coptpy as cp

    env = cp.Envr()
    model = env.createModel("Q3_Aircraft_Assignment")
    # 时段离散化 (分钟): 06:00=360 到 18:00=1080，步长 15min
    slots = list(range(360, 1080, 15))
    y = {}
    for a in aircraft_list:
    for r_id, r in enumerate(required_sorties):
    if r["airport"] != a["home_airport"]:
    continue
    for t in slots:
    if t >= 360 and (t + r["duration"]) <= 1200:
    y[(a["id"], r_id, t)] = model.addVar(
    vtype=cp.COPT.BINARY, obj=r["duration"],
    name=f"y_{a['id']}_{r_id}_{t}")
    # 每架次恰好指派一次
    for r_id in range(len(required_sorties)):
    vars_r = [v for (aid, rid, t), v in y.items() if rid == r_id]
    if vars_r:
    model.addConstr(cp.quicksum(vars_r) == 1, name=f"assign_{r_id}")
    # 同机不重叠 + 30min 周转 (简化：按时段互斥，用 time-slot 冲突约束省略，靠 obj 求最小)
    # 完备约束：对同机 a 的任意两候选 (r,t) 与 (r',t') 若 t+dur+30 > t' 则互斥
    for a in aircraft_list:
    combos = [(r_id, t) for (aid, r_id, t) in y if aid == a["id"]]
    for i in range(len(combos)):
    for j in range(i + 1, len(combos)):
    r1, t1 = combos[i]
    r2, t2 = combos[j]
    dur1 = required_sorties[r1]["duration"]
    if not (t1 + dur1 + 30 <= t2 or t2 + required_sorties[r2] ["duration"] + 30 <= t1):
    model.addConstr(y[(a["id"], r1, t1)] + y[(a["id"], r2, t2)] <= 1)
    model.setParam(cp.COPT.Param.TimeLimit, time_limit)
    t0 = time.time()
    model.solve()
    status = model.status
    obj = model.objval if status in (0, 1) else None
    assigned = {}
    if status in (0, 1):
    for (aid, r_id, t), v in y.items():
    try:
    if v.x > 0.5:
    assigned[r_id] = (aid, t)

# =---- 集成点 3：临时任务背包
def solve_temporary_knapsack(temp_persons, delta_times, T_base, T_current, time_limit=120.0):
    """阶段二：预算内最大化 temporary 履约（方案 §三(三)）。

    delta_times: {pid: ΔT} (零增量直插 = 0)
    返回 (accepted_pids, obj, status)。

    """
    import coptpy as cp

    env = cp.Envr()
    model = env.createModel("Q3_Temporary_Knapsack")
    z = {}
    for pid in temp_persons:
    z[pid] = model.addVar(vtype=cp.COPT.BINARY, obj=1.0, name=f"z_{pid}")
    budget = max(T_base - T_current, 0.0)
    if temp_persons:
    model.addConstr(
    cp.quicksum(delta_times.get(pid, 1e9) * z[pid] for pid in temp_persons) <= budget,
    name="time_budget")
    model.setObjective(cp.quicksum(z[pid] for pid in temp_persons), sense=cp.COPT.MAXIMIZE)
    model.setParam(cp.COPT.Param.TimeLimit, time_limit)
    t0 = time.time()
    model.solve()
    status = model.status
    accepted = []
    if status in (0, 1):
    for pid in temp_persons:
    try:
    if z[pid].x > 0.5:
    accepted.append(pid)
    except Exception:
    pass
    return accepted, model.objval if status in (0, 1) else None, status, time.time() - t0

def solve_temporary_assignment(options, sorties, time_limit=120.0):
    """Select zero-duration-increment temporary insertions with route-segment capacities."""
    import coptpy as cp

    env = cp.Envr()
    model = env.createModel("Q3_Temporary_Assignment")
    z = {index: model.addVar(vtype=cp.COPT.BINARY, obj=1.0, name=f"z_{index}")
    for index in range(len(options)))
    by_person = {}
    for index, option in enumerate(options):
    by_person.setdefault(option["person_id"], []).append(index)
    for pid, option_ids in by_person.items():
    model.addConstr(cp.quicksum(z[index] for index in option_ids) <= 1, name=f"temp_once_{pid}")

def _route_load(sortie, segment):
    if sortie.get("loads") is not None and segment < len(sortie["loads (
    return sortie["loads"][segment]
    route = [sortie["airport"]] + sortie["stops"] + [sortie["airport"]
    load = 0
    for pid_list in sortie.get("passengers", {}).values(:
    load += len(pid_list)
    # The detailed interval capacity is enforced by the Q3 pre-filter. This
    # conservative base load keeps the COPT insertion model safe when a route
    # has mixed pickup/drop-off streams.
    return min(load, AIRCRAFT[sortie["aircraft_type"]].seats)

# =================== 集成点 4/5：下界 Benchmark
def lower_bound_benchmark(candidate_sorties, all_pids, time_limit=60.0):
    """小规模子集精确下界（论文 gap 证据）。返回 (obj, status, elapsed)。"""
    selected, obj, status, elapsed = solve_set_partitioning(candidate_sorties, all_pids, time_limit)
    return obj, status, elapsed

    print(f" [COPT 精化] status={status} obj={obj:.0f} 选中 {len(selected)}/{len(cands)} 梁次 ({elapsed:.1f}s}")
    sorties = selected
    else:
    print(f" [回退] COPT 未出解 (status={status}), 用初解")

# 4. 导出 + 校验
out_dir = RESULTS / "q2"
out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
from pipeline import write_q1q2
write_q1q2(sorties, people, out_dir, qname="q2")
errs = validate_routes_q1q2(out_dir / "q2-routes.csv", len(people), out_dir / "q2-assignments.csv")

## A.2.13 metrics\_q12.py（78行）——Q1/Q2 五项指标统计

```python
"""Q1/Q2 五项指标计算（题目口径）。

Q1/Q2 的 routes 无时刻字段：按 06:00 起飞构造每架次的确定时刻表
(Q1/Q2 无时间窗，等待为 0，时刻只用于在途时间口径)，飞行时间 ceil(dist/speed*60)，每停靠 10/20 分钟（加油 20）。

指标：
    总架次数、总飞机使用时间、人员总在途时间、总燃油消耗、座位利用率。
"""

import csv
import math
import sys
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from common import AIRCRAFT, flight_time, load_distances, load_people

def q12_metrics(routes_path, assignments_path, q):
    _, dist = load_distances()
    people = load_people(q)
    person_map = {p["person_id']: p for p in people}
    routes = list(csv.DictReader(open(routes_path, encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    assigns = list(csv.DictReader(open(assignments_path, encoding="utf-8-sig")))
    by_key = defaultdict(list)
    for r in routes:
    by_key[(r["aircraft_type"], r["flight_no"])].append(r)

    total_use = 0.0
    total_fuel = 0.0
    total_seat_km = 0.0
```

```python
total_pax_km = 0.0
travel = {}
for key, rows in by_key.items():
    rows = sorted(rows, key=lambda r: int(r["stop_order"])
    route = [r["facility_id"] for r in rows]
    refuel = {int(r["stop_order"]), r["refuel"] == "1" for r in rows}
    ac = AIRCRAFT[key[0]]
# 确定时刻：06:00 起飞，飞行 ceil，停靠 dwell
times = {0: 360.0}
current = 360.0
for i in range(len(route) - 1):
    current += flight_time(dist[(route[i], route[i + 1])], ac)
    if i + 1 < len(route) - 1:
    current += 20 if refuel.get(i + 1) else 10
    times[i + 1] = current
    total_use += current - 360.0
# 段載客
loads = [0] * (len(route) - 1)
for a in assigns:
    if a["aircraft_type"] == key[0] and a["flight_no"] == key[1]:
    pu, de = int(a["pickup_stop_order (
    int(a["delivery_stop_order"])
    for seg in range(pu, de):
    loads[seg] += 1
    travel[a["person_id"]] = times[de] - times[pu]
    for i in range(len(route) - 1):
    d = dist[(route[i], route[i + 1]])
    total_fuel += d * ac.fuel_rate
    total_seat_km += d * ac.seats
    total_pax_km += d * loads[i]
covered = sum(1 for a in assigns if a["aircraft_type"])
return {
    "总架次数": len(by_key),
    "总飞机使用时间(min)": round(total_use),
    "人员总在途时间(min)": round(sum(travel.values()))
    "总燃油消耗(kg)": round(total_fuel, 1),
    "座位利用率(%)": round(total_pax_km / total_seat_km * 100, 2) if total_seat_km else 0,
    "覆盖": covered,
    "需求": len(people),
}

if __name__ == "__main__":
    rp = Path(sys.argv[1])
    ap = Path(sys.argv[2])
    q = int(sys.argv[3])
print(q12_metrics(rp, ap, q))
```

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file Channel).resolve().parent))
from common import AIRCRAFT, load_distances

TIME_FMT = "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"

def q3_metrics(routes_path, assignments_path):
    _, dist = load_distances()
    routes = list(csv.DictReader(open(routes_path, encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    assigns = list(csv.DictReader(open(assignments_path, encoding="utf-8-sig")))
    by_key = defaultdict(list)
    for r in routes:
    by_key[(r["aircraft_id"], r["flight_no"])].append(r)

    total_use = 0
    total_fuel = 0.0
    total_seat_km = 0.0
    total_pax_km = 0.0
    total_travel = 0
    covered = 0
    for (aircraft_id, flight_no), rows in by_key.items():
    rows = sorted(rows, key=lambda r: int(r["stop_order"])
    route = [r["facility_id"] for r in rows]
    ac = AIRCRAFT[aircraft_id.split("-")[1]]
    start = datetime.strptime(rows[0]["departure_time"], TIME_FMT)
    end = datetime.strptime(rows[-1]["arrival_time"], TIME_FMT)
    total_use += (end - start).total_seconds() / 60
    loads = [0] * (len(route) - 1)
    interval = defaultdict(list)
    for a in assigns:
    if a["aircraft_id"] == aircraft_id and a["flight_no"] == flight_no:
    pu, de = int(a["pickup_stop_order (
    int(a["delivery_stop_order"])
    interval[(a["person_id"])] = (pu, de)
    for seg in range(pu, de):
    loads[seg] += 1
    covered += 1
    for i in range(len(route) - 1):
    d = dist[(route[i], route[i + 1]])
    total_fuel += d * ac.fuel_rate
    total_seat_km += d * ac.seats
    total_pax_km += d * loads[i]

# 在途时间
person_travel = {}
for a in assigns:
    if not a["aircraft_id"]:
    continue
    rows = sorted(by_key[(a["aircraft_id"], a["flight_no"])], key=lambda r: int(r["stop_order"]))
    dep = datetime.strptime(rows[int(a["pickup_stop_order"]]) ["departure_time"], TIME_FMT)
    arr = datetime.strptime(rows[int(a["delivery_stop_order"]]) ["arrival_time"], TIME_FMT)
    person_travel[a["person_id"] = (arr - dep).total_seconds() / 60
    total_travel = sum(person_travel.values())
    return {
    "总架次数": len(by_key),
    "总飞机使用时间(min)": round(total_use),
    "总燃油消耗(kg)": round(total_fuel, 1),

    fleet.append({"id": f"{airport}-{ac_type}-H{serial:02d}",
"home_airport": airport, "type": ac_type})
    return fleet

def load_q3() -> list[dict]:
    people = load_people(3)
    for person in people:
    person["E"] = datetime.strptime(person["earliest_pickup_time"],
```

TIME_FMT)
    person["L"] = datetime.strptime(person["latest_arrival_time"], TIME_FMT)
    person["priority"] = PRIORITY.get(person["task_type"], 3)
    return people

def _candidate_airports(person: dict, dist: dict) -> list[str]:
    """Return all legally usable home airports, ordered by direct proximity."""
    origin, destination = person["origin_id"], person["destination_id"]
    if origin.startswith("A"):
    return [origin]
    if destination.startswith("A"):
    return [destination]
    target = destination if origin == "LAND" else origin
    return sorted(AIRPORTS, key=lambda airport: dist[(airport, target)])

def _route_intervals(sortie: dict, person_map: dict[str, dict]) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]] | None:
    route = [sortie["airport"]] + sortie["stops"] + [sortie["airport"]]
    index = {node: i for i, node in enumerate(route)}
    intervals = []
    for pid in sortie["pids"]:
    person = person_map[pid]
    origin, destination = person["origin_id"], person["destination_id"]
    pickup = 0 if origin == "LAND" or origin.startswith("A") else
index.get(origin)
    delivery = len(route) - 1 if destination == "LAND" or
destination.startswith("A") else index.get(destination)
    if pickup is None or delivery is None or pickup >= delivery:
    return None
    if origin.startswith("A") and route[0] != origin:
    return None
    if destination.startswith("A") and route[-1] != destination:
    return None
    intervals.append((pid, pickup, delivery))
    return intervals

def _batch_candidates(eligible: list[dict], dist: dict, fleet: list[dict], day: date) -> list[dict]:
    """Create endpoint batches, compatible same-direction merges and safe singletons."""
    person_map = {person["person_id']: person for person in eligible}
    by_signature: dict[tuple, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    for person in eligible:
    airport = _home_airport(person, dist)
    kind = _kind(person)
    if kind == "out":
    signature = (airport, kind, person["destination_id"])
    elif kind == "in":
    signature = (airport, kind, person["origin_id"])
    else:
    signature = (airport, kind, person["origin_id"],
    person["destination_id"])
    by_signature[signature].append(person["person_id"])
    available_types = defaultdict(list)
    for aircraft in fleet:
    available_types[aircraft["home_airport"].append(aircraft["type"])
    chunks: list[tuple[tuple, list[str]]] = []

def _schedule_candidate(candidate: dict, day: date, fleet: list[dict],
next_available: dict[str, datetime], person_map: dict[str, dict], dist: dict) -> bool:
    intervals = _route_intervals(candidate, person_map)

    times[stop_index] = (arrival, next_departure)
    current = next_departure
    if current > day_return:
    continue
    feasible = True
    for pid, pickup, delivery in intervals:
    pickup_time = times[pickup][1]
    delivery_time = times[delivery][0]

def _candidate_feasible_on_day(candidate: dict, day: date, person_map: dict[str, dict], dist: dict) -> bool:
    aircraft_id = f"{candidate['airport']}-{candidate['aircraft_type']}H99"
    fleet = [{"id": aircraft_id, "home_airport": candidate['airport'], "type": candidate['aircraft_type']}]
    next_available = {aircraft_id: datetime.combine(day, time.min) + timedelta(minutes=DAY_START)}
    probe = dict(candidate, pids=list(candidate["pids"], passengers={key: list(value) for key, value in candidate["passengers"].items()})
    return _schedule_candidate(probe, day, fleet, next_available, person_map, dist)

def _fixed_schedule(candidate: dict, day: date, ac_type: str, start_minute: int, person_map: dict[str, dict], dist: dict):
    intervals = _route_intervals(candidate, person_map)
    if intervals is None:
    return None
    route = [candidate["airport"]] + candidate["stops"] + [candidate["airport"]] pickup_pids = defaultdict(list)
    for pid, pickup, _ in intervals:
    pickup_pids[pickup].append(pid)
    departure = datetime.combine(day, time.min) + timedelta(minutes=start_minute)
    if departure.hour * 60 + departure.minute > DAY_LAST_DEPARTURE:
    return None
    for pid in pickup_pids.get(0, []):
    if departure < person_map[pid]["E"]:
    return None
    times = {0: (None, departure)}
    current = departure
    for index, (origin, destination) in enumerate(zip(route, route[1]:)):
    arrival = current + timedelta(minutes=flight_time(dist[(origin, destination)], AIRCRAFT[ac_type]))
    stop_index = index + 1

    score = sum(priority_weight[person_map[pid]["task_type"]] for pid in variant["pids"]) - 0.001 * fixed["duration"]
    options.append({

return options

def _assign_daily_options(selected: list[dict], day: date, fleet: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    next_available = {aircraft["id']: datetime.combine(day, time.min) + timedelta(minutes=DAY_START) for aircraft in fleet}
    assigned = []
    for option in sorted(selected, key=lambda item: (item["start"], item["occupied_end"])):
    aircraft_options = [aircraft for aircraft in fleet if aircraft["home_airport"] == option["airport"] and aircraft["type"] == option["aircraft_type"] and next_available[aircraft["id"]] <= option["start"]]
    if not aircraft_options:
    continue
    aircraft = min(aircraft_options, key=lambda item: (next_available[item["id"]], item["id"]))
    sortie = dict(option["candidate"])
    sortie.update({"aircraft_id": aircraft["id"], "times": option["times"], "day": day, "duration": option["duration"], "intervals": option["intervals"], "loads": option["loads"]})
    next_available[aircraft["id"]] = option["occupied_end"]
    assigned.append(sortie)
    return assigned

def _try_schedule(candidate: dict, day: date, fleet: list[dict], next_available: dict[str, datetime], person_map: dict[str, dict], dist: dict) -> list[dict]:
    type_order = [candidate["aircraft_type"]] + [ac_type for ac_type in ("T1", "T2", "T3") if ac_type != candidate["aircraft_type"]]
    passenger_stops = list(dict.fromkeys(node for pid in candidate["pids"] for node in _passenger_stops(person_map[pid])))
    for ac_type in type_order:
    variant = _make_candidate(candidate["airport"], candidate["pids"], passenger_stops, person_map, dist, [ac_type])
    if variant is not None and _schedule_candidate(variant, day, fleet, next_available, person_map, dist):
    return [variant]
    if len(candidate["pids"]) <= 1:
    return []
    scheduled = []
    for pid in sorted(candidate["pids"], key=lambda item: (person_map[item]["priority"], person_map[item]["L"])):
    singleton = _make_candidate(candidate["airport"], [pid], _passenger_stops(person_map[pid]), person_map, dist, [candidate["aircraft_type"]])
    if singleton is not None and _schedule_candidate(singleton, day, fleet, next_available, person_map, dist):
    scheduled.append(singleton)
    return scheduled

```python
def _temporary_options(temporary: list[dict], sorties: list[dict], person_map: dict[str, dict], dist: dict) -> list[dict]:
    options = []
    for person in temporary:
    for route_index, sortie in enumerate(sorties):
    intervals = _route_intervals(sortie, person_map)
    if intervals is None:
    continue
    trial = dict(sortie)
    trial["pids"] = list(sortie["pids"]) + [person["person_id"]]
    trial_intervals = _route_intervals(trial, person_map)
    if trial_intervals is None:
    continue
    new_interval = next((item for item in trial_intervals if item[0] == person["person_id"]), None)
    if new_interval is None:
    continue
    _, pickup, delivery = new_interval
    times = sortie["times"]
    if times[pickup][1] < person["E"] or times[delivery][0] >
    person["L"]:
    continue
    loads = [0] * (len(sortie["stops"]) + 1)
    for _, old_pickup, old_delivery in intervals:
    for segment in range(old_pickup, old_delivery):
    loads[segment] += 1
    for segment in range(pickup, delivery):
    loads[segment] += 1
    if any(load > AIRCRAFT[sortie["aircraft_type"]].seats for load in loads):
    continue
    options.append({"person_id": person["person_id"], "route_index": route_index, "pickup": pickup, "delivery": delivery})
    return options

def add_temporary_with_copt(temporary: list[dict], sorties: list[dict], person_map: dict[str, dict], dist: dict, time_limit: float = 120.0) -> tuple[list[str], int, int, float]:
    from copt_solver import solve_temporary_assignment
    options = _temporary_options(temporary, sorties, person_map, dist)
    selected, objective, status, elapsed = solve_temporary_assignment(options, sorties, time_limit=time_limit)
    for option in selected:
    sortie = sorties[option["route_index"]]
    pid = option["person_id"]
    sortie["pids"].append(pid)
    route = [sortie["airport"]] + sortie["stops"] + [sortie["airport"]]
    sortie.setdefault("passengers", {}).setdefault(route[option["pickup"]], []).append(pid)
    if sortie.get("loads") is None:
    sortie["loads"] = [0] * (len(route) - 1)
    for segment in range(option["pickup"], option["delivery"])
    sortie["loads"][segment] += 1
    return [option["person_id"] for option in selected], objective, status, elapsed
```

def _repair_with_singleton_sorties(people: list[dict], sorties: list[dict],
    person_map: dict[str, dict], fleet:
    list[dict],
    dist: dict) -> list[str]:
    """Place the final uncovered mandatory demands into concrete aircraft gaps."""

    start = sortie["times"][0][1]
    end = sortie["times"][len(sortie["stops"]) + 1][0] +
    timedelta(minutes=TURNAROUND)
    busy[(sortie["aircraft_id"], start.date())].append((start, end))
    repaired = []
    for person in sorted(people, key=lambda item: (item["priority"], item["L"], item["E"])):
    options = []

    aircraft["home_airport"] == airport]

    aircraft_at_airport}):

    for stops, refuel in _fuel_variants(
    airport, _passenger_stops(person), ac, dist):
    route = [airport] + stops + [airport]
    pickup = 0 if person["origin_id"] == "LAND" or

    delivery = len(route) - 1 if person["destination_id"] == "LAND" or person["destination_id"].startswith("A") else
    route.index(person["destination_id"])

    candidate = {

    day = person["E"].date()
    while day <= person["L"].date():
    start_minutes = set(range(DAY_START, DAY_LAST_DEPARTURE + 1, 5))
    if day == person["E"].date():
    start_minutes.add(person["E"].hour * 60 +
    person["E"].minute)
    for start_minute in sorted(start_minutes):

    start = datetime.combine(day, time.min) +
    timedelta(minutes=start_minute)
    end = fixed["occupied_end"]
    for aircraft in aircraft_at_airport:
    if aircraft["type"] != ac_type:
    continue
    conflicts = busy[(aircraft["id"], day)]
    if any(not (end <= old_start or old_end <= start)
    for old_start, old_end in conflicts):

def write_outputs(sorties: list[dict], people: list[dict], out_dir: Path) -> None:
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    people_map = {person["person_id']: person for person in people}
    sorties = sorted(sorties, key=lambda sortie: (sortie["aircraft_id"], sortie["times"][0][1]))
    flight_numbers: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    assignment_map = {}
    route_rows = []
    for sortie in sorties:
    aircraft_id = sortie["aircraft_id"]
    flight_numbers[aircraft_id] += 1
    flight_no = flight_numbers[aircraft_id]
    route = [sortie["airport"]] + sortie["stops"] + [sortie["airport"]]
    for index, facility in enumerate(route):
    arrival, departure = sortie["times"][index]
    route_rows.append({
    "aircraft_id": aircraft_id,
    "flight_no": flight_no,
    "stop_order": index,
    "facility_id": facility,
    "arrival_time": arrival.strftime(TIME_FMT) if arrival else "",
    "departure_time": departure.strftime(TIME_FMT) if departure else "",
    "refuel": "1" if sortie.get("refuel", {}).get(index) else "0",
    })
    intervals = _route_intervals(sortie, people_map) or []
    for pid, pickup, delivery in intervals:
    assignment_map[pid] = (aircraft_id, flight_no, pickup, delivery)
    with (out_dir / "q3-routes.csv").open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
    writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=["aircraft_id", "flight_no", "stop_order", "facility_id", "arrival_time", "departure_time", "refuel"])
    writer.writeheader()
    writer.writerows(route_rows)
    with (out_dir / "q3-assignments.csv").open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
```

def solve_instance(limit: int | None = None, time_limit: float = 120.0) -> tuple[list[dict], list[dict], dict]:
    nodes, dist = load_distances()
    people = load_q3()
    if limit is not None:
    people = people[:limit]
    fleet = generate_fleet()
    sorties, unscheduled = solve_non_temporary(people, dist, fleet)
    person_map = {person["person_id']: person for person in people}
    repair_people = [person_map[pid] for pid in sorted(unscheduled)]
    repaired, _, _, _ = add_temporary_with_copt(
    repair_people, sorties, person_map, dist, time_limit)
    unscheduled.difference_update(repaired)
    singleton_repaired = _repair_with_singleton_sorties(
    [person_map[pid] for pid in sorted(unscheduled)], sorties, person_map, fleet, dist)
    unscheduled.difference_update(singleton_repaired)
    second_repair, _, _, _ = add_temporary_with_copt(
    [person_map[pid] for pid in sorted(unscheduled)], sorties, person_map, dist, time_limit)
    unscheduled.difference_update(second_repair)
    retimed_repair = _retime_insert_mandatory(
    [person_map[pid] for pid in sorted(unscheduled)], sorties, person_map, dist)
    unscheduled.difference_update(retimed_repair)
    rerouted_repair = _reroute_insert_mandatory(
    [person_map[pid] for pid in sorted(unscheduled)], sorties, person_map, dist)
    unscheduled.difference_update(rerouted_repair)
    temporary = [person for person in people if person["task_type"] == "temporary"]
    temp_selected, temp_obj, temp_status, temp_elapsed = add_temporary_with_copt(temporary, sorties, person_map, dist, time_limit)
    summary = {
    "non_temporary": len(people) - len(temporary),
    "non_temporary_scheduled": len(people) - len(temporary) - len(unscheduled),
    "non_temporary_unscheduled": len(unscheduled),
    "temporary_selected": len(temp_selected),
    "temporary_copt_objective": temp_obj,
    "temporary_copt_status": temp_status,
    "temporary_copt_elapsed": temp_elapsed,
    "base_time_min": sum(sortie["duration"] for sortie in sorties),
    "sorties": len(sorties),
}
```

```python
return sorties, people, summary

def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=120.0)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=None,
    help="结果目录；小样本默认写入 results/q3-samples")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    sorties, people, summary = solve_instance(args.limit, args.timeout)
    print(f"Q3 summary: {summary}")
    out_dir = args.out or (RESULTS / "q3" if args.limit is None
    else RESULTS / "q3-samples" / f"limit-{args.limit}")
    write_outputs(sorties, people, out_dir)
    if args.limit is None:
    from strict_validator import validate_q3
    report = validate_q3(out_dir / "q3-routes.csv", out_dir / "q3-assignments.csv")
    print(f"STRICT_Q3 {'PASS' if report['ok'] else 'FAIL'}
    routes={report['routes']} assigned={report['assigned']}/{report['demand']}")
    for error in report["errors"][:20]:
    print(f" {error}")
    return 0 if report["ok"] else 1
    return 0

if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

## A.2.16 q3\_lower\_bound.py（61行）——Q3 列生成下界

```python
"""Q3 下界实验：用 Q2 候选池覆盖 Q3 全部 mandatory (3840 人),
忽略时间窗、24 机机队与运营时段约束 → Q3 原问题的有效下界（受限列池口径）。

Q2 池 = build_route_pool(8 rounds) + 停靠顺序扩池 (solver-benchmark 缓存)。
同时输出 LP 松弛值（同样是当前列池口径的下界，不是全局证明）。

"""

import pickle
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from common import load_people

BASE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
pool_path = BASE / "results" / "experiments" / "solver-benchmark" /
"pool_q2.pkl"
with open(pool_path, "rb") as f:
    all_pids, rows = pickle.load(f)

people3 = load_people(3)
mandatory = {p["person_id"] for p in people3 if p["task_type"] != "temporary"}
print(f"mandatory={len(mandatory)} pool_columns={len(rows)}")

# 覆盖约束：mandatory 恰好一次；temporary 无约束（顺带服务不影响下界）
import coptpy as cp
```

```python
env = cp.Envr()
model = env.createModel("Q3_lower_bound_no_time")
x = [model.addVar(vtype=cp.COPT.BINARY, name=f"x{i}") for i in range(len(rows))]
covering = {pid: [] for pid in mandatory}
for i, (_, pids) in enumerate(rows):
    for pid in pids:
    if pid in covering:
    covering[pid].append(i)
missing = [pid for pid, idx in covering.items() if not idx]
print("missing mandatory:", len(missing), missing[:5])
for pid, idx in covering.items():
    model.addConstr(cp.quicksum(x[i] for i in idx) == 1, name=f"c{pid}")
model.setObjective(cp.quicksum(rows[i][0] * x[i] for i in range(len(rows))), sense=cp.COPT.MINIMIZE)
model.setParam(cp.COPT.Param.TimeLimit, 120)
model.setParam(cp.COPT.Param.RelGap, 0.0)
t0 = time.time()
model.solve()
elapsed = time.time() - t0
print(f"MIP status={model.status} obj={model.objval} elapsed={elapsed:.2f}s")
try:
    print(f"bestbound={model.bestbound}")
except Exception as e:
    print("no bestbound attr:", e)

# LP 松弛下界（重新建模为连续变量）
env2 = cp.Envr()
m2 = env2.createModel("Q3_lb_LP")
y = [m2.addVar(lb=0.0, ub=1.0, vtype=cp.COPT.CONTINUOUS, name=f"y{i}") for i in range(len(rows))]
for pid, idx in covering.items():
    m2.addConstr(cp.quicksum(y[i] for i in idx) == 1, name=f"c{pid}")
m2.setObjective(cp.quicksum(rows[i][0] * y[i] for i in range(len(rows))), sense=cp.COPT.MINIMIZE)
m2.setParam(cp.COPT.Param.TimeLimit, 60)
t0 = time.time()
m2.solve()
print(f"LP status={m2.status} obj={m2.objval} elapsed={time.time()-t0:.2f}s")
```

## A.2.17 q3\_repair\_milp.py（430行）——Q3 临时任务联合修复 MILP

```txt
q3_repair_milp.py - Q3 重定时-局部改线联合修复 MILP（创新 B）
把第一版的两个贪心函数（_retime_insert_mandatory / _reroute_insert_mandatory）
替换为一个小规模 COPT 联合模型：
决策：
x_r ∈ {0,1} 是否用修复列 r 替换其宿主架次（每宿主至多一个版本）
t_r 连续 修复列起飞分钟（1 分钟精度重定时）
arr/dep/wait 连续 停靠时刻与等待（吸收 pickup 最早时间）
邻域（列 r 在模型外预生成并验证）：
宿主架次 + 未覆盖乘客 → 在合法位置插入其停靠点（≤5 停靠、燃油/座位
复验、穿梭 0 在 d 前）+ 加油变体。同时允许"只重定时不改线"列。
同机同日互斥用 pairwise big-M disjunction；规模被列裁剪控制（每个未覆盖者
```

```python
最多 3 个宿主 × 2 拓扑), COPT 秒级求解。

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from collections import defaultdict
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from common import (AIRCRAFT, FUEL_STATIONS, MAX_STOPS, dp_load_check,
    flight_time, fuel_check, sortie_duration)
from pipeline_support import _fuel_variants, _kind, _passenger_stops

BIG_M = 3000.0
DAY_START = 360
DAY_LAST_DEPARTURE = 1080
DAY_RETURN = 1200
TURNAROUND = 30
EPS = 1e-6

# ---- 列生成
def _insert_topologies(sortie, person, person_map, dist):
    """在保留宿主现有停靠顺序的前提下，枚举插入 person 停靠点的合法拓扑。

    返回 [(stops, refuel, aircraft_type, intervals, fixed_duration)]。
    intervals 为全部乘客的 (pid, pickup, delivery)。
    """
    pids = list(sortie["pids"])
    if person["person_id"] in pids or len(pids) + 1 > AIRCRAFT["T3"].seats:
    return []
    stops = list(sortie["stops"])
    kind = _kind(person)
    need_o = person["origin_id"] if person["origin_id"].startswith("F") else None
    need_d = person["destination_id"] if person["destination_id"].startswith("F") else None
    airport = sortie["airport"]
    route_stop_lists = []
    if kind == "out":
    if person["destination_id"] in stops:
    route_stop_lists.append(stops)
    else:
    for i in range(1, len(stops) + 2):
    trial = stops[:i - 1] + [person["destination_id"]] + stops[i - 1:]
    route_stop_lists.append(trial)
    elif kind == "in":
    if person["origin_id"] in stops:
    route_stop_lists.append(stops)
    else:
    for i in range(1, len(stops) + 2):
    trial = stops[:i - 1] + [person["origin_id"]] + stops[i - 1:]
    route_stop_lists.append(trial)
    else: # shuttle: o 在 d 前
    o, d = person["origin_id"], person["destination_id"]
    if o in stops and d in stops:
    if stops.index(o) < stops.index(d):
    route_stop_lists.append(stops)

def generate_repair_columns(unscheduled, sorties, person_map, dist, max_hosts=3, max_topos=2):
    """为每个未覆盖者生成有限修复列集合。

    返回 {(p, r_id): column}; column 包含 host_index 或 None。
    列在进入 MILP 前必须通过 15 分钟粒度的固定时刻可行性探针
    （时间窗 + 运营时段），避免零增量但时间窗不可行的拓扑占掉名额。
    """
    from q3_solve import _fixed_schedule
    columns = []
    host_extra = {}  # r_id -> (dur_host, orig_start_min, aircraft_id, day)
    for person in unscheduled:
    pid = person["person_id"]
    candidates = []
    for s_index, sortie in enumerate(sorties):
    if sortie["airport"] not in _candidate_airports(person, dist):
    continue
    day = sortie["times"][0][1].date()
    if not (person["E"].date() <= day <= person["L"].date()):
    continue
    topos = _insert_topologies(sortie, person, person_map, dist)
    if not topos:
    continue
    feasible_topos = []
    same_aircraft = sorted(
    (item for item in sorties
    if item["aircraft_id"] == sortie["aircraft_id"]
    and item["times"][0][1].date() == day),
    key=lambda item: item["times"][0][1],
    )

return columns, host_extra

def _candidate_airports(person, dist):
    from q3_solve import _candidate_airports as f
    return f(person, dist)

# ---- MILP
def solve_repair_milp(columns, host_extra, sorties, person_map, dist, time_limit=120.0):
    """联合选择修复列 + 连续重定时。返回 (selected_r_ids, status, objval, elapsed)。"""
    import coptpy as cp

    n = len(columns)
    if n == 0:
    return [], None, None, 0.0
    env = cp.Envr()
    model = env.createModel("Q3_Retime_Reroute_Repair")

    x = {r: model.addVar(vtype=cp.COPT.BINARY, name=f"x_{r}") for r in range(n)}
    t = {r: model.addVar(lb=0, ub=1440, vtype=cp.COPT.CONTINUOUS, name=f"t_{r}")
    for r in range(n)}
    arr = {}
    dep = {}
    wait = {}
    shift = {}
    for r, column in enumerate(columns):
    m = len(column["stops"]) + 1
    for i in range(m + 1):
    arr[(r, i)] = model.addVar(lb=0, ub=BIG_M, vtype=cp.COPT.CONTINUOUS, name=f"arr_{r}_{i}")
    dep[(r, i)] = model.addVar(lb=0, ub=BIG_M, vtype=cp.COPT.CONTINUOUS, name=f"dep_{r}_{i}")
    wait[(r, i)] = model.addVar(lb=0, ub=BIG_M, vtype=cp.COPT.CONTINUOUS, name=f"w_{r}_{i}")
    shift[r] = model.addVar(lb=0, ub=BIG_M, vtype=cp.COPT.CONTINUOUS, name=f"shift_{r}")

# ---- 时间传播 (big-M 激活)
for r, column in enumerate(columns):
    ac = AIRCRAFT[column["aircraft_type"]
    route = [column["airport"]) + column["stops"] + [column["airport"]]
    m = len(route) - 1
    xr = x[r]
    model.addConstr(dep[(r, 0)] >= t[r] - BIG_M * (1 - xr), name=f"dep0lo_{r}")
    model.addConstr(dep[(r, 0)] <= t[r] + BIG_M * (1 - xr), name=f"dep0up_{r}")
```

```python
model.addConstr(wait[(r, 0)] == 0)
model.addConstr(wait[(r, m)] == 0)
for i in range(m):
    ft = flight_time(dist[(route[i], route[i + 1])], ac)
    model.addConstr(arr[(r, i + 1)] >= dep[(r, i)] + ft - BIG_M * (1 - xr), name=f"arrlo_{r}_{i}")
    model.addConstr(arr[(r, i + 1)] <= dep[(r, i)] + ft + BIG_M * (1 - xr), name=f"arrup_{r}_{i}")
    if 0 < i + 1 < m:
    dwell = 20 if column["refuel"].get(i + 1) else 10
    model.addConstr(dep[(r, i + 1)] >= arr[(r, i + 1)] + dwell + wait[(r, i + 1)] - BIG_M * (1 - xr),
    name=f"deplo_{r}_{i}")
    model.addConstr(t[r] >= DAY_START - BIG_M * (1 - xr), name=f"tlo_{r}")
    model.addConstr(t[r] <= DAY_LAST_DEPARTURE + BIG_M * (1 - xr),
    name=f"tup_{r}")
    model.addConstr(arr[(r, m)] <= DAY_RETURN + BIG_M * (1 - xr),
    name=f"ret_{r}")
    # 原起飞偏移（目标防无谓移动）
    _, orig_start_dt, _, day = host_extra[r]
    orig_min = orig_start_dt.hour * 60 + orig_start_dt.minute
    model.addConstr(shift[r] >= t[r] - orig_min - BIG_M * (1 - xr),
    name=f"shiftlo_{r}")
    model.addConstr(shift[r] >= orig_min - t[r] - BIG_M * (1 - xr),
    name=f"shiftup_{r}")
    # 时间窗
    for pid, pickup, delivery in column["intervals"]:
    person = person_map[pid]
    if person["E"].date() == day:
    e_min = person["E"].hour * 60 + person["E"].minute
    model.addConstr(dep[(r, pickup)] >= e_min - BIG_M * (1 - xr),
    name=f"E_{r}_{pid}")
    if person["L"].date() == day:
    l_min = person["L"].hour * 60 + person["L"].minute
    model.addConstr(arr[(r, delivery)] <= min(l_min, DAY_RETURN) + BIG_M * (1 - xr), name=f"L_{r}_{pid}")

# 每宿主至多一个修复版本
by_host = defaultdict(list)
for r, column in enumerate(columns):
    by_host[column["host_index"].append(r)
for r_list in by_host.values():
    model.addConstr(cp.quicksum(x[r] for r in r_list) <= 1,
    name=f"host_{r_list[0]}")

# 每人至多一列（hard），覆盖在目标层用大惩罚体现
by_person = defaultdict(list)
for r, column in enumerate(columns):
    by_person[column["p"].append(r)
for pid, r_list in by_person.items():
    model.addConstr(cp.quicksum(x[r] for r in r_list) <= 1,
    name=f"person_{pid}")
    cover_expr = cp_linExpr()
    for r in range(n):
    cover_expr += x[r]

# 同机同日互斥
by_resource = defaultdict(list)
for r, column in enumerate(columns):
    by_resource[(column["aircraft_id"], column["day"])].append(r)
```

```python
pair_disj = {}
    fixed_units = defaultdict(list)  # (aircraft, day) -> [(start_min, end_min_with_turn)]
    for s_index, sortie in enumerate(sorties):
    day = sortie["times"][0][1].date()
    start_min = sortie["times"][0][1].hour * 60 + sortie["times"][0][1].minute
    end_min = sortie["times"][len(sortie["stops"]) + 1][0].hour * 60 + sortie["times"][len(sortie["stops"]) + 1][0].minute
    fixed_units[(sortie["aircraft_id"], day)].append((s_index, start_min, end_min + TURNAROUND))

    for resource, r_list in by_resource.items():
    for a in range(len(r_list)):
    for b in range(a + 1, len(r_list)):
    r1, r2 = r_list[a], r_list[b]
    if columns[r1]["host_index"] == columns[r2]["host_index"]:
    continue  # 同宿主已被 host 约束互斥
    y = model.addVar(vtype=cp.COPT.BINARY, name=f"y_{r1}_{r2}")
    pair_disj[(r1, r2)] = y
    model.addConstr(t[r1] + (arr[(r1, len(columns[r1]["stops"]) + 1)] - t[r1]) + TURNAROUND - t[r2]
    <= BIG_M * (1 - y) + BIG_M * (2 - x[r1] - x[r2]), name=f"disj_a_{r1}_{r2}")
    model.addConstr(t[r2] + (arr[(r2, len(columns[r2]["stops"]) + 1)] - t[r2]) + TURNAROUND - t[r1]
    <= BIG_M * y + BIG_M * (2 - x[r1] - x[r2]), name=f"disj_b_{r1}_{r2}")
    # 修复列 VS 未修改固定架次
    for s_index, start_min, end_min in fixed_units[resource]:
    for r in r_list:
    if columns[r]["host_index"] == s_index:
    continue
    y = model.addVar(vtype=cp.COPT.BINARY, name=f"fy_{r}_{s_index}")
    model.addConstr(t[r] + (arr[(r, len(columns[r]["stops"]) + 1)] - t[r]) + TURNAROUND - start_min
    <= BIG_M * (1 - y) + BIG_M * (1 - x[r]), name=f"fx_a_{r}_{s_index}")
    model.addConstr(end_min - t[r] <= BIG_M * y + BIG_M * (1 - x[r]), name=f"fx_b_{r}_{s_index}")

# ---- 目标：覆盖优先（大惩罚）→机时增量 →偏移稳定
# 线性化：g_r = max(dur_r - t_r - host_dur, 0)（仅当 x=1 时有意义）
g = {}
for r in range(n):
    host_dur = host_extra[r][0]
    m = len(columns[r]["stops"]) + 1
    g[r] = model.addVar(lb=0, ub=BIG_M, vtype=cp.COPT.CONTINUOUS, name=f"g_{r}")
    model.addConstr(g[r] >= arr[(r, m)] - t[r] - host_dur - BIG_M * (1 - x[r]), name=f"glo_{r}")
    total_increment = cp.LinExpr()
    total_shift = cp.LinExpr()
    for r in range(n):
    total_increment += g[r]
    total_shift += shift[r]
    objective = 1e6 * (len(by_person) - cover_expr) + total_increment + 1e-3 * total_shift
    model.setObjective(objective, sense=cp.COPT.MINIMIZE)
    model.setParam(cp.COPT.Param.TimeLimit, time_limit)
```

```python
model.setParam(cp.COPT.Param.RelGap, 0.0)
import time
t0 = time.time()
model.solve()
elapsed = time.time() - t0
status = model.status
if status not in (cp.COPT.OPTIMAL):
    return [], status, None, elapsed
selected = []
for r in range(n):
    try:
    if x[r].x > 0.5:
    selected.append((r, round(t[r].x)))
except Exception:
    pass
return selected, status, model.objval, elapsed

def apply_repair(sorties, columns, host_extra, selected, person_map, dist):
    """把MILP选中的列写回sorties。selected = [(r_id, start_min)]。"""
    covered = []
    for r, start_min in selected:
    column = columns[r]
    host_index = column["host_index"]
    sortie = sorties[host_index]
    day = sortie["times"][0][1].date()
    from q3_solve import _fixed_schedule
    fixed = _fixed_schedule(column, day, column["aircraft_type"], start_min, person_map, dist)
    if fixed is None:
    continue
    sortie.update({
    "stops": list(column["stops (
    "refuel": dict(column["refuel (
    "aircraft_type": column["aircraft_type"],
    "pids": list(column["pids (
    "times": fixed["times"],
    "duration": fixed["duration"],
    "intervals": fixed["intervals"],
    "loads": fixed["loads"],
    })
    covered.append(column["p"])
    return covered

def run_repair(unscheduled, sorties, person_map, dist, time_limit=120.0):
    """对外入口：生成列→解MILP→应用。返回(repaired_pids, report)。"""
    columns, host_extra = generate_repair_columns(unscheduled, sorties, person_map, dist)
    report = {"columns": len(columns), "unscheduled_in": len(unscheduled)}
    if not columns:
    return [], report
    selected, status, objval, elapsed = solve_repair_milp(columns, host_extra, sorties, person_map, dist, time_limit)
    report.update({"status": status, "objval": objval, "elapsed": elapsed})
    repaired = apply_repair(sorties, columns, host_extra, selected, person_map, dist)
    report["repaired"] = len(repaired)
    return repaired, report
```

## A.2.18 q3\_retime\_lp.py（289行）——Q3 整日重定时 LP

```python
q3_retime_lp.py - Q3 整日连续重定时抛光（创新 B1）

在保持路线、乘客与同机架次顺序不变的条件下，对每天每架次以 1 分钟精度连续调整起飞时刻，最小化总飞机使用时间。

模型是纯 LP（保持同机顺序则无互斥二元变量）：
    start_s    : 梁次起飞分钟（连续）
    arr_{s,i}, dep_{s,i}: 各停靠到达/出发分钟
    wait_{s,i} >= 0    : 停靠等待（用于吸收 pickup 最早时间）
    shift_s    : 与原起飞时刻的绝对偏移（目标中极小权重，防无谓移动）

约束：
    dep_{s,0} = start_s
    arr_{s,i+1} = dep_{s,i} + flight_time(s, i)
    dep_{s,i} >= arr_{s,i} + dwell(s, i)
    dep_{s,i} >= E_p    (p 在 i 上机)
    arr_{s,j} <= L_p    (p 在 j 下机)
    start_s >= 360, start_s <= 1080, arr_{s,last} <= 1200
    同机顺序：start_{k+1} >= arr_{k,last} + 30

用法：
    python q3_retime_lp.py --routes <q3-routes.csv> --assignments <q3-assignments.csv>
    --out <dir> [--log lp]

def _parse_aircraft_id(aircraft_id: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """A01-T2-H03 -> ('A01', 'T2')"""
    parts = aircraft_id.split Flo(-")

    return parts[0], parts[1]

AIRCRAFT[sortie["aircraft_type"]]) if 0 < index < len(route) - 1: duration += 20 if sortie["refuel"].get(index) else 10

def _sortie_key(sortie: dict) -> tuple:
    return (sortie["aircraft_id"], sortie["times"][0][1], sortie.get("flight_no", 0))

def retime_day(sorties: list Yoiclist), person_map: dict, dist: dict, use_integer: bool = False) -> dict:
    """整日连续重定时。保持每架次路线与每机架次顺序。返回（优化后 sorties，报告）。””
    import coptpy as cp

    sorties = sorted(sorties, key=_sortie_key)
    env = cp.Envr()
    model = env.createModel("Q3_Day_Retime")
    n = len(sorties)

# 决策变量（分钟；纯连续 LP，系数全整数）
start = {}
arr = {}
dep = {}
wait = {}
shift = {}
vtype = cp.COPT.INTEGER if use_integer else cp.COPT.CONTINUOUS
for s_index, sortie in enumerate(sorties):
    route = [sortie["airport"]] + sortie["stops"] + [sortie["airport"]] m = len(route) - 1
    start[s_index] = model.addVar(lb=DAY_START, ub=DAY_LAST_DEPARTURE, vtype=vtype, name=f"start_{s_index}")
    for i in range(m + 1):
    arr[(s_index, i)] = model.addVar(lb=0, ub=1440 + 120, vtype=vtype, name=f"arr_{s_index}_{i}")
    dep[(s_index, i)] = model.addVar(lb=0, ub=1440 + 120, vtype=vtype, name=f"dep_{s_index}_{i}")
    wait[(s_index, i)] = model.addVar(lb=0, ub=720, vtype=vtype, name=f"wait_{s_index}_{i}")
    shift[s_index] = model.addVar(lb=0, ub=720, vtype=vtype, name=f"shift_{s_index}")

# 约束
for s_index, sortie in enumerate(sorties):
    route = [sortie["airport"]] + sortie["stops"] + [sortie["airport"]] ac = AIRCRAFT[sortie["aircraft_type"]] m = len(route) - 1
    model.addConstr(dep[(s_index, 0)] == start[s_index], name=f"dep0_{s_index}")
    model.addConstr(wait[(s_index, 0)] == 0, name=f"wait0_{s_index}")
    model.addConstr(wait[(s_index, m)] == 0, name=f"wait_last_{s_index}")
    for i in range(m):
    ft = flight_time(dist[(route[i], route[i + 1])], ac)
    model.addConstr(arr[(s_index, i + 1)] == dep[(s_index, i)] + ft, name=f"arr_{s_index}_{i + 1}")
```

```python
if 0 < i + 1 < m:
    dwell = 20 if sortie["refuel"].get(i + 1) else 10
    model.addConstr(dep[(s_index, i + 1)] >= arr[(s_index, i + 1)] + dwell + wait[(s_index, i + 1)],
    name=f"dep_{s_index}_{i + 1}")

# 迟场上限
model.addConstr(arr[(s_index, m)] <= DAY_RETURN,
name=f"return_{s_index}")

# 人员时间窗（仅当天生效；跨天窗口由运营时段约束覆盖）
day = sortie["times"][0][1].date()
for pid, pickup, delivery in sortie["intervals"]:
    person = person_map[pid]
    if person["E"].date() == day:
    e_min = person["E"].hour * 60 + person["E"].minute
    model.addConstr(dep[(s_index, pickup)] >= e_min,
name=f"E_{s_index}_{pid}")

if person["L"].date() == day:
    l_min = person["L"].hour * 60 + person["L"].minute
    model.addConstr(arr[(s_index, delivery)] <= min(l_min,
DAY_RETURN), name=f"L_{s_index}_{pid}")

# 与原起飞时刻的偏移（用于防无调移动）
orig_min = sortie["times"][0][1].hour * 60 + sortie["times"][0][1].minute
model.addConstr(shift[s_index] >= start[s_index] - orig_min,
name=f"shiftp_{s_index}")
model.addConstr(shift[s_index] >= orig_min - start[s_index],
name=f"shiftm_{s_index}")

# 同机顺序 + 周转（仅同一天内相邻杂次；跨天由次日 06:00 起降自然隔开）
by_aircraft: dict[str, list[int]] = defaultdict(list)
for s_index, sortie in enumerate(sorties):
    by_aircraft[sortie["aircraft_id"]].append(s_index)
for indices in by_aircraft.values():
    by_day: dict = defaultdict(list)
    for s_index in indices:
    by_day[sorties[s_index]["times"][0][1].date()] .append(s_index)
    for same_day in by_day.values():
    for prev, next_in zip(same_day, same_day[1 Multi]:):
    prev_last = len(sorties[prev]["stops"]) + 1
    model.addConstr(start[next_] >= arr[(prev, prev_last)] +
TURNAROUND, name=f"turn_{prev}_{next}_")

# 目标：总使用时间 + le-4 * 偏移（保持时间轴稳定）
total_duration = cp.LinExpr()
total_shift = cp.LinExpr()
for s_index, sortie in enumerate(sorties):
    m = len(sortie["stops"]) + 1
    total_duration += arr[(s_index, m)] - start[s_index]
    total_shift += shift[s_index]
model.setObjective(total_duration + le-4 * total_shift,
sense=cp.COPT.MINIMIZE)
model.setParam(cp.COPT.Param.TimeLimit, 300)
t0 = __import__(“time”).time()
model.solve()
elapsed = __import__(“time”).time() - t0
status = model.status

report = {"status": status, "objval": None, "elapsed": elapsed, "before": sum(s["duration"] for s in sorties), "after": None}
if status not in (cp.COPT.OPTIMAL,:

if i < m else None)

def round_sortie_times(sorties: list[dict], person_map: dict, dist: dict) -> bool:
    """把 LP 时间四舍五入后按确定性规则重算固定时刻表，保证业务约束逐条满足。"""
    ok = True
    for sortie in sorties:
    route = [sortie["airport"]] + sortie["stops"] + [sortie["airport"]]
    start_dt = sortie["times"][0][1]
    day = start_dt.date()
    start_min = start_dt.hour * 60 + start_dt.minute
    from q3_solve import _fixed_schedule
    fixed = _fixed_schedule(sortie, day, sortie["aircraft_type"], start_min, person_map, dist)
    if fixed is None:
    ok = False
    continue
    sortie["times"] = fixed["times"]
    sortie["duration"] = fixed["duration"]
    sortie["intervals"] = fixed["intervals"]
    sortie["loads"] = fixed["loads"]
    return ok

def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--routes", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--assignments", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--integer", action="store_true", help="整数起飞变量 (MIP, 慢但免取整) ")
    args = parser.parse_args()

_, dist = load_distances()
people = load_people(3)
for person in people:
    person["E"] = datetime.strptime(person["earliest_pickup_time"], TIME_FMT)
    person["L"] = datetime.strptime(person["latest_arrival_time"], TIME_FMT)
person_map = {person["person_id']: person for person in people}

use_integer=args.integer)
print(f"retime status={report['status']} before={report['before']} after={report['after']} elapsed={report['elapsed']:.2f}s")
if report["after"] is not None:
    round_sortie_times(sorties, person_map, dist)
    print(f"after-round: {sum(s['duration'] for s in sorties)}")
if args.out is not None:

routes={report['routes']} assigned={report['assigned']}/{report['demand']}")

    return 0 if report["ok"] else 1
return 0

if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

## A.2.19 q3\_stage\_b\_experiment.py（307行）——Q3 Stage-B 局部压缩与对照实验

```python
q3_stage_b_experiment.py - Q3 ε-约束 Stage-B 局部实验 (V3 P1)

以 v2 最终排班（mandatory-only 基准 100534、temporary 158/160）为输入：
1. repair_probe: 把未覆盖 temporary (P3015/P3246) 交给 q3_repair_milp, 记录最小联合增量（不限制预算）。
2. order_savings: 对现有架次做「真实停靠顺序局部搜索」寻找可压缩的路线时间（固定机型、保持乘客 OD 合法），并验证同机前后序可行性。
3. 若 savings ≥ 插入增量，则应用节省 + 插入 + 整日重定时，尝试 159/160。

实验只写新结果目录，不修改 v2 正式结果。

from q3_repair_milp import run_repair

```python
from alns_v3 import best_order_sortie_v3
from alns_v2 import _legal_orders
from pipeline_support import (_make_candidate_preserve_order_refuel,
    _passenger_stops)

TIME_FMT = "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"
DAY_START = 360
DAY_LAST_DEPARTURE = 1080
DAY_RETURN = 1200
TURNAROUND = 30

def total_time(sorties):
    return sum(s["duration"] for s in sorties)

def repair_probe(pids, sorties, person_map, dist, time_limit=120.0):
    """联合修复 MILP 对 temporary 的最小增量探针（不限制总机时预算）。"""
    before = total_time(sorties)
    repaired, report = run_repair(
    [person_map[pid] for pid in pids], sorties, person_map, dist,
time_limit=time_limit)
    after = total_time(sorties)
    report.update({"probe_pids": pids, "before_time": before, "after_time": after,
    "delta_time": after - before})
    return repaired, report

def _neighbor_bounds(sorties, sortie):
    day = sortie["times"][0][1].date()
    same = sorted((s for s in sorties
    if s["aircraft_id"] == sortie["aircraft_id"] and s["times"]
[0][1].date() == day),
    key=lambda s: s["times"][0][1])
    position = next(i for i, s in enumerate(same) if s is sortie)
    earliest = datetime.combine(day, time.min) + timedelta(minutes=DAY_START)
    latest = datetime.combine(day, time.min) + timedelta(minutes=DAY_RETURN)
    if position > 0:
    previous_return = same[position - 1]["times"][len(same[position - 1]
["stops"]) + 1][0]
    earliest = max(earliest, previous_return +
timedelta(minutes=TURNAROUND))
    if position + 1 < len(same):
    latest = same[position + 1]["times"][0][1]
    return earliest, latest

def order_savings_probe(sorties, person_map, dist, max_sorties=200):
    """对最多 max_sorties 个架次做真实顺序局部搜索，返回可压缩候选。"""
    candidates = []
    ordered = sorted(sorties, key=lambda s: -(len(s["pids"]) * 3 +
len(s["stops"])))
    checked = 0
    for sortie in ordered:
    if len(sortie["stops"]) < 2 or len(sortie["pids"]) < 2:
    continue
    improved = best_order_sortie_v3(sortie, person_map, dist,
    allowed_types=[sortie["aircraft_type"]],
    with_refuel=True)
```

    report["applied_changes"] = [c for p in report["passes"] for c in
p["applied"]
    report["applied_savings"] = round(sum(p["applied_savings"] for p in
report["passes"]), 1)

    if temp_pids:
    repaired2, probe2 = repair_probe(list(temp_pids), sorties, person_map,
dist)
    report["repair_probe_after_savings"] = {
    k: (str(v) if isinstance(v, (datetime, time)) else v) for k, v in
probe2.items()}
    report["repair_probe_after_savings"]["repaired"] = list(repaired2)

    report["final_time"] = total_time(sorties)
    return sorties, report

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--routes", type=Path, required=True)
```

## A.2.20 strict\_validator.py（301行）——三问独立严格校验器（STRICT\_Q1/Q2/Q3）

```python
"""Independent strict validators for the 2026 contest B problem outputs."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import re
from collections import defaultdict
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

from common import AIRCRAFT, FUEL_STATIONS, MAX_STOPS, flight_time,
load_distances, load_people

TIME_FMT = "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"
TIME_RE = re.compile(r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2} \d{2}:\d{2}$")
AIRPORTS = {"A01", "A02", "A03"}
FLEET_LIMITS = {
    "A01": {"T1": 3, "T2": 3, "T3": 2},
    "A02": {"T1": 2, "T2": 4, "T3": 2},
    "A03": {"T1": 2, "T2": 3, "T3": 3},
}

def read_rows(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as handle:
    return list(csv.DictReader(handle))

def parse_time(value: str) -> datetime:
    return datetime.strptime(value, TIME_FMT)

def validate_route(route: list[str], refuel: dict[int, int], ac_type: str, dist: dict) -> list[str]:
    errors: list[str] = []
    if not route or route[0] != route[-1] or route[0] not in AIRPORTS:
    errors.append("route must start and end at the same airport")
    if sum(node.startswith("F") for node in route) > MAX_STOPS:
    errors.append("more than five offshore landings")
    if ac_type not in AIRCRAFT:
    return errors + [f"unknown aircraft type {ac_type}]
    for index, node in enumerate(route):
    flag = refuel.get(index, 0)
    if flag not in (0, 1):
    errors.append(f"invalid refuel flag at stop {index}")
    if node in AIRPORTS and flag:
    errors.append(f"airport refuel flag must be zero at stop {index}")
    if flag and node not in FUEL_STATIONS:
    errors.append(f"refuel at non-refuel facility {node}")
    fuel = AIRCRAFT[ac_type].tank
    for index, (origin, destination) in enumerate(zip(route, route[1]:)):
    fuel -= dist[(origin, destination)] * AIRCRAFT[ac_type].fuel_rate
```

```python
if fuel < AIRCRAFT[ac_type].reserve - 1e-7:
    errors.append(f"fuel reserve violation {origin} -> {destination}")
if refuel.get(index + 1, 0):
    fuel = AIRCRAFT[ac_type].tank
return errors

def validate_assignment(person: dict, route: list[str], pickup: int, delivery: int) -> str | None:
    if not (0 <= pickup < delivery < len(route)):
    return "pickup/delivery order or range is invalid"
    origin, destination = person["origin_id"], person["destination_id"]
    if origin == "LAND":
    if pickup != 0 or route[pickup] not in AIRPORTS:
    return "LAND origin must board at the departure airport"
    elif route[pickup] != origin:
    return "pickup facility differs from origin"
    if destination == "LAND":
    if delivery != len(route) - 1 or route[delivery] not in AIRPORTS:
    return "LAND destination must alight at the return airport"
    elif route[delivery] != destination:
    return "delivery facility differs from destination"
    return None

def validate_q12(routes_path: Path, assignments_path: Path, q: int) -> dict:
_, dist = load_distances()
people = load_people(q)
person_map = {row["person_id']: row for row in people}
errors: list[str] = []
groups: dict[tuple[str, str], list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)
for row in read_rows(routes_path):
    groups[(row.get("aircraft_type", "", row.get("flight_no", "")]).append(row)
route_info: dict[tuple[str, str], dict] = {}
for key, group in groups.items():
    try:
    ordered = sorted(group, key=lambda row: int(row["stop_order"]))
    orders = [int(row["stop_order"]) for row in ordered]
    refuel = {int(row["stop_order"]) int(row.get("refuel", "0")) for row in ordered}
except (KeyError, ValueError):
    errors.append(f"route {key}: invalid integer field")
    continue
if orders != list(range(len(orders)):
    errors.append(f"route {key}: stop_order is not consecutive")
route = [row["facility_id"] for row in ordered]
errors.extend(f"route {key}: {message}" for message in validate_route(route, refuel, key[0], dist))
route_info[key] = {"route": route, "type": key[0], "loads": [0] * (len(route) - 1)}
assignments = read_rows(assignments_path)
counts: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
for row in assignments:
    pid = row.get("person_id", "")
    if pid not in person_map:
    errors.append(f"unknown person {pid}")
    continue
    counts[pid] += 1
    key = (row.get("aircraft_type", "", row.get("flight_no", ""))
```

    errors.append(f"{pid}: references missing sortie {key}")
    continue
    try:

except (KeyError, ValueError):

    message = validate_assignment(person_map[pid], route_info[key]["route"], pickup, delivery)

    for pid in person_map:

    for key, info in route_info.items():

    by_type: dict[str, set[int]] = defaultdict(set)

    errors.append(f"{ac_type}: flight_no is not consecutive")
    return {"ok": not errors, "errors": errors, "routes": len(route_info), "assigned": len(assignments), "demand": len(people)}

def validate_q3(routes_path: Path, assignments_path: Path) -> dict:
    _, dist = load_distances()
    people = load_people(3)
    for row in people:
    row["E"] = parse_time(row["earliest_pickup_time"])
    row["L"] = parse_time(row["latest_arrival_time"])
    person_map = {row["person_id']: row for row in people}
    errors: list[str] = []
    groups: dict[tuple[str, str], list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)
    for row in read_rows(routes_path):
    groups[(row.get("aircraft_id", ""), row.get("flight_no", "")]append(row)
    route_info: dict[tuple[str, str], dict] = {}
    by_aircraft: dict[str, list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)
    for key, group in groups.items():
    aircraft_id, _ = key
    match = re.match(r"^(A0[123])-(T[123])-H(\d{2})$", aircraft_id)
    if not match:
    errors.append(f"invalid aircraft_id {aircraft_id}")

    parser = argparse()

    return 0 if report["ok"] else 1

if __name__ == "__main__":
