## A.2.21 validate\_q3.py（81行）——Q3 结果结构校验

```python
"""
validate_q3.py - Q3 格式校验器 (aircraft_id/时刻/停靠/覆盖)

q3-routes.csv:
aircraft_id, flight_no, stop_order, facility_id, arrival_time, departure_time, refuel
- aircraft_id 格式 A0x-Tx-Hxx; flight_no 按飞机从 1 连续
- 架次首行仅 departure_time; 末行仅 arrival_time; 中间双时刻
- 时刻 YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM; 首尾同机场; 海上停靠≤5; refuel 规则同 q1
q3-assignments.csv:
person_id, aircraft_id, flight_no, pickup_stop_order, delivery_stop_order
- 4000 行; 未安排的保留 person_id 后四列空

"""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
```

```python
import re
import sys
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
from common import FUEL_STATIONS, RESULTS

TIME_RE = re.compile(r"\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2} \d{2}:\d{2}$")
AIRCRAFT_RE = re.compile(r"^A0[123]-T[123]-H\d{2}$")

def validate(path_routes: Path, path_assign: Path, n_people: int) -> list[str]:
    errs = []
    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open(path_routes, encoding="utf-8")))
    if not rows:
    return ["routes 为空"]
    # aircraft_id 格式
    for r in rows:
    if not AIRCRAFT_RE.match(r["aircraft_id"]);
    errs.append(f"aircraft_id 格式: {r['aircraft_id']}")
    break
    t = r.get("arrival_time") or r.get("departure_time")
    if t and not TIME_RE.match(t):
    errs.append(f"时刻格式: {t}")
    break
    # 架次结构
    sorties = defaultdict(list)
    for r in rows:
    sorties[(r["aircraft_id"], r["flight_no"])].append(r)
    for (aid, fno), stops in sorties.items():
    ss = sorted(stops, key=lambda x: int(x["stop_order"])
    facs = [s["facility_id"] for s in ss]
    if facs[0] != facs[-1]:
    errs.append(f"{aid}-{fno}: 首尾不一致")
    if len([f for f in facs if f.startswith("F")]) > 5:
    errs.append(f"{aid}-{fno}: 海上停靠 >5")
    # 首行仅 dep、末行仅 arr
    first, last = ss[0], ss[-1]
    if not first["departure_time"] or first["arrival_time"]:
    errs.append(f"{aid}-{fno}: 首行应仅 departure_time")
    if not last["arrival_time"] or last["departure_time"]:
    errs.append(f"{aid}-{fno}: 末行应仅 arrival_time")
    # flight_no 连续
    per_ac = defaultdict(set)
    for r in rows:
    per_ac[r["aircraft_id"]].add(r["flight_no"])
    for aid, nos in per_ac.items():
    got = sorted(int(n) for n in nos)
    if got != list(range(1, len(nos) + 1)):
    errs.append(f"{aid}: flight_no 不连续 {got[:5]}")

# assignments
arrows = list(csv.DictReader(open(path_assign, encoding="utf-8")))
if len(arrows) != n_people:
    errs.append(f"assignments 行数 {len(arows)} != {n_people}")
covered = sum(1 for r in arows if r["aircraft_id"])
errs.append(f"覆盖 {covered}/{n_people} (前三类应全覆盖；temp 可留空) ")
return errs
```

```python
if __name__ == "__main__":
    errs = validate(RESULTS / "q3" / "q3-routes.csv", RESULTS / "q3" / "q3-assignments.csv", 4000)
    hard = [e for e in errs if not e.startswith("覆盖")]
    print("Q3 校验:", "☑ 格式合规" if not hard else f" {len(hard)} 问题")
    for e in errs:
    print(" ", e)
```

## A.2.22 test\_q3\_retime.py（101行）——重定时 LP 单元测试

```python
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import unittest

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

from common import AIRCRAFT, flight_time, load_distances
from q3_solve import (_fixed_schedule, _reroute_insert_mandatory,
    _retime_insert_mandatory, load_q3)

def _scheduled_sortie(airport, stops, refuel, ac_type, pids, day, start_minute,
    person_map, dist, aircraft_id="A03-T3-H01"):
    candidate = {
    "airport": airport,
    "stops": list(stops),
    "refuel": dict(refuel),
    "aircraft_type": ac_type,
    "pids": list(pids),
    "passengers": {},
    }
    fixed = _fixed_schedule(candidate, day, ac_type, start_minute, person_map,
dist)
    assert fixed is not None
    candidate.update(fixed)
    candidate["aircraft_id"] = aircraft_id
    candidate["day"] = day
    return candidate

class RetimeMandatoryTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_retime_insert_keeps_duration_and_respects_following_sortie(self):
    people = load_q3()
    person_map = {person["person_id']: person for person in people}
    _, dist = load_distances()
    p3717 = person_map["P3717"]
    p3846 = person_map["P3846"]
    day = datetime(2026, 8, 6).date()

    first = _scheduled_sortie(
    "A03", ["F044", "F037"], {1: True}, "T3", [p3717["person_id"]],
```

## A.2.23 solver\_benchmark.py（175行）——三求解器同池对比基准

```python
python
solver_benchmark.py - 同一列池、同参数口径下 COPT / SCIP / HiGHS 集合划分 MIP 对比。

模型：min Σ c_r x_r s.t. Σ a_pr x_r = 1 ∀p, x∈{0,1}
列池：v2 口径 = build_route_pool(rounds=8) + 停靠顺序枚举扩池 (Q1: 1871 列；Q2: 4921 列)。
参数：时间上限 300s，MIP 相对 gap 0.001。
输出：results/solver-benchmark/benchmark.csv + 打印摘要。

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from common import load_distances, load_people
from pipeline_support import build_route_pool
from pipeline_v2 import expand_pool_by_order
from ftsg import build_ftsg

def build_pool(q: int, cache_dir: Path):
    cache = cache_dir / f"pool_q{q}.pkl"
    if cache.exists():
    import pickle
    with open(cache, "rb") as f:
    return pickle.load(f)
    _, dist = load_distances()
    people = load_people(q)
    ftsg = build_ftsg()
    cands = build_route_pool(people, dist, rounds=8, use_ftsg=True)
    extra = expand_pool_by_order(cands, people, dist, ftsg)
    cands = cands + extra
    all_pids = [p["person_id"] for p in people]
    # 标准化列数据
    rows = []
    for c in cands:
    pids = list(c["pids"])
    if not pids or len(pids) != len(set(pids)):
    continue
    rows.append((c["duration"], pids))
    import pickle
    with open(cache, "wb") as f:
    pickle.dump((all_pids, rows), f)
    return all_pids, rows

def solve_copt(all_pids, rows, time_limit=300.0, rel_gap=0.001):
    import coptpy as cp
    env = cp.Envr()
    model = env.createModel("bench")
    x = [model.addVar(vtype=cp.COPT.BINARY, name=f"x{i}") for i, (dur, _) in enumerate(rows)]
    covering = {pid: [] for pid in all_pids}
    for i, (_, pids) in enumerate(rows):
    for pid in pids:
```

```python
covering[pid].append(i)
for pid, idx in covering.items():
    model.addConstr(cp.quicksum(x[i] for i in idx) == 1, name=f"c{pid}")
model.setObjective(cp.quicksum(dur * x[i] for i, (dur, _) in enumerate(rows)), sense=cp.COPT.MINIMIZE)
model.setParam(cp.COPT.Param.TimeLimit, time_limit)
model.setParam(cp.COPT.Param.RelGap, rel_gap)
t0 = time.time()
model.solve()
elapsed = time.time() - t0
rec = {"status": model.status, "obj": model.objval if model.status in (1,)
else None,
    "elapsed": elapsed}
for attr in ("bestbound", "BestObj", "bestobj", "miprelgap", "relgap"):
    if hasattr(model, attr):
    try:
    rec[attr] = getattr(model, attr)
    except Exception:
    pass
    try:
    rec["gap"] = abs(model.bestbound - model.objval) /
max(abs(model.objval), 1e-9) if model.status in (1,) else None
except Exception:
    pass
return rec

def solve_scip(all_pids, rows, time_limit=300.0, rel_gap=0.001):
    from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum
    model = Model("bench")
    model.hideOutput(True)
    model.setRealParam("limits/time", time_limit)
    model.setRealParam("limits/gap", rel_gap)
    x = [model.addVar(vtype="B") for _ in rows]
    covering = {pid: [] for pid in all_pids}
    for i, (_, pids) in enumerate(rows):
    for pid in pids:
    covering[pid].append(i)
    for pid, idx in covering.items():
    model.addCons(quicksum(x[i] for i in idx) == 1, f"c{pid}")
    model.setObjective(quicksum(dur * x[i] for i, (dur, _) in enumerate(rows)), "minimize")
    t0 = time.time()
    model.optimize()
    elapsed = time.time() - t0
    status = model.status()
    rec = {"status": status, "elapsed": elapsed}
    try:
    rec["obj"] = model.getObjVal()
except Exception:
    rec["obj"] = None
try:
    rec["gap"] = model.getGap()
except Exception:
    rec["gap"] = None
try:
    rec["primalbound"] = model.getPrimalbound()
    rec["dualbound"] = model.getDualbound()
except Exception:
    pass
```

```python
return rec

def solve_highs(all_pids, rows, time_limit=300.0, rel_gap=0.001):
    import highspy
    h = highspy.Highs()
    h.setOptionValue("output_flag", False)
    h.setOptionValue("time_limit", time_limit)
    h.setOptionValue("mip_rel_gap", rel_gap)
    x = [h.addVariable(lb=0, ub=1, type=highspy.HighsVarType.kInteger) for _ in rows]
    covering = {pid: [] for pid in all_pids}
    for i, (_, pids) in enumerate(rows):
    for pid in pids:
    covering[pid].append(i)
    for pid, idx in covering.items():
    expr = sum(x[i] for i in idx)
    h.addConstr(expr == 1)
    objective = sum(dur * x[i] for i, (dur, _ ) in enumerate(rows))
    h.setObjective(objective, sense=highspy.ObjSense.kMinimize)
    t0 = time.time()
    h.run()
    elapsed = time.time() - t0
    status = str(h.getModelStatus())
    rec = {"status": status, "elapsed": elapsed}
    try:
    rec["obj"] = h.getObjectiveValue()
    except Exception:
    rec["obj"] = None
    try:
    info = h.getInfo()
    rec["gap"] = getattr(info, "mip_gap", None)
    rec["primalbound"] = getattr(info, "mip_primal_bound", None)
    rec["dualbound"] = getattr(info, "mip_dual_bound", None)
    except Exception:
    pass
    return rec

def main():
    out_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "results" / "experiments" / "solver-benchmark"
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    results = []
    for q in (1, 2):
    print(f"[bench] Q{q} build pool ..., flush=True)
    all_pids, rows = build_pool(q, out_dir)
    print(f"[bench] Q{q} pool={len(rows)} rows={len(all_pids)}", flush=True)
    for name, solver in ("COPT", solve_copt), ("SCIP", solve_scip), ("HiGHS", solve_highs)):
    print(f"[bench] Q{q} {name} solving ..., flush=True)
    rec = solver(all_pids, rows)
    rec.update({"q": q, "solver": name, "columns": len(rows), "demands": len(all_pids)})
    results.append(rec)
    print(f" {rec}", flush=True)
    with open(out_dir / "benchmark.csv", "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=["q", "solver", "columns", "demands", "status", "obj", "gap", "elapsed"])
```

```python
w.writeheader()
for rec in results:
    w.writerow({k: rec.get(k) for k in w.fieldnames})
print("saved", out_dir / "benchmark.csv")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

from __future__ import annotations

RESULTS_DIR = PAPER / "submission" / "XXX_支撑材料" / "results"

from common import load_distances, load_people, AIRCRAFT, fuel_check,
```

```python
plt.rcParams["font.family"] = "sans-serif"
plt.rcParams["font.sans-serif"] = ["SimHei", "SimSun", "Microsoft YaHei"]
CB_COLORS = ["#0072B2", "#D55E00", "#009E73", "#CC79A7", "#E69F00", "#56B4E9"]
REFUEL = {"F006", "F011", "F018", "F024", "F031", "F038", "F044", "F050"}

def _load():
    nodes, dist = load_distances()
    people = {q: load_people(q) for q in (1, 2, 3)}
    airports = [n for n in nodes if n.startswith("A")]
    facilities = [n for n in nodes if n.startswith("F")]
    return nodes, dist, people, airports, facilities

def _mds_coords(nodes, dist):
    from sklearn.manifold import MDS
    matrix = np.zeros((len(nodes), len(nodes)))
    for i, a in enumerate(nodes):
    for j, b in enumerate(nodes):
    matrix[i, j] = dist.get((a, b), dist.get((b, a), 0.0))
    coords = MDS(n_components=2, dissimilarity="precomputed", random_state=42, normalized_stress="auto", n_init=4, max_iter=500).fit_transform(matrix)
    return {nodes[i]: coords[i] for i in range(len(nodes))}

def _save(fig, name, prefix):
    FIG_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out = FIG_DIR / f"{prefix}{name}.png"
    fig.savefig(out, dpi=300, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close(fig)
    return out

def _read_csv(path):
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as stream:
    return list(csv.DictReader(stream))

def _routes(question):
    if question in (1, 2):
    return _read_csv(RESULTS_DIR / f"v4-final-q{question}" / f"q{question}-routes.csv")
    return _read_csv(RESULTS_DIR / f"v3-final-q{question}" / f"q{question}-routes.csv")

def _group_sorties(rows):
    groups = defaultdict(list)
    for row in rows:
    key = (row.get("aircraft_type") or re.search(r"(T[123])",
    row.get("aircraft_id", "").group(1),
    row["flight_no"])
    groups[key].append(row)
    return groups

def _fact_metrics():
    text = (MATERIALS_DIR / "01-结果汇总.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    metrics = {}
```

```python
labels = {
    "总飞机使用时间 (min)": "duration",
    "人员总在途时间 (min)": "travel",
    "总架次数": "sorties",
    "总燃油消耗 (kg)": "fuel",
    "座位利用率 (%)": "utilization",
}
for source_label, key in labels.items():
    line = next((line for line in text.splitlines() if line.startswith(f" | {source_label})), None)
    if line is None:
    raise RuntimeError(f"V3 fact row missing: {source_label}")
    cells = [cell.strip() for cell in line.strip("|").split("|")]
    metrics[key] = {}
    for q, cell in zip((1, 2, 3), cells[1:4]):
    numbers = [float(x) for x in re.findall(r"\d+(?:.\d+)?", cell)]
    # 事实源口径：v4 最终 / v3 最终 / v2 基线
    metrics[key][q] = {"v4": numbers[0], "v3": numbers[1], "v2": numbers[2]}
    return metrics

def _report(question):
    if question in (1, 2):
    return json.loads((RESULTS_DIR / f"v4-final-q{question}" / "v4_report.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return json.loads((RESULTS_DIR / f"v3-final-q{question}" / f"v3_report.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

def fig_network(prefix=""):
    nodes, dist, _, airports, facilities = _load()
    coords = _mds_coords(nodes, dist)
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(14 / 2.54, 10.5 / 2.54))
    ax.scatter([coords[f][0] for f in facilities], [coords[f][1] for f in facilities],
    s=24, c=CB_COLORS[0], label="海上设施")
    ax.scatter([coords[a][0] for a in airports], [coords[a][1] for a in airports],
    s=70, marker="^", c=CB_COLORS[1], label="陆地机场")
    for node in sorted(REFUEL):
    ax.scatter(*coords[node], s=62, marker="o", facecolors="none", edgecolors=CB_COLORS[4], linewidths=1.5)
    ax.annotate(node, coords[node], xytext=(4, 4), textcoords="offset points", fontsize=8)
    for airport in airports:
    ax.annotate(airport, coords[airport], xytext=(5, 5), textcoords="offset points", fontsize=9)
    ax.set_title("机场、海上设施与可加油设施分布")
    ax.legend(fontsize=9)
    ax.set_aspect("equal")
    return _save(fig, "network", prefix)

def fig_heatmap(prefix=" ):
    nodes, dist, _, _, _ = _load()
    matrix = np.array([[dist.get((a, b), dist.get((b, a), 0.0)) for b in nodes] for a in nodes])
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(13.5 / 2.54, 11 / 2.54))
    image = ax.imshow(matrix, cmap="viridis", aspect="auto")
```

```python
    _, _, people, _, _ = _load()
    fig, (ax1, ax2) = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(14 / 2.54, 6.2 / 2.54))

def _route_check(route, ac_type, dist):
    aircraft = AIRCRAFT[ac_type]
    ok, fuel, remain = fuel_check(route, aircraft, dist)
    return ok, fuel, remain, sortie_duration(route, aircraft, dist)

def fig_routes(prefix=""):
    nodes, dist, _, airports, facilities = _load()
    coords = _mds_coords(nodes, dist)
    routes = [("A01", "T2", ["A01", "F017", "F030", "F014", "A01"]), ("A02", "T2", ["A02", "F017", "F030", "A02"]), ("A03", "T3", ["A03", "F017", "F020", "A03"])]
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(14 / 2.54, 10.5 / 2.54))
    ax.scatter([coords[f][0] for f in facilities], [coords[f][1] for f in facilities], s=15, c="#cccccc")
    for i, (airport, ac_type, route) in enumerate(routes):
    _, fuel, _, duration = _route_check(route, ac_type, dist)
    xs, ys = zip(*(coords[node] for node in route))
    ax.plot(xs, ys, marker="o", lw=2, color=CB_COLORS[i], label=f"{airport}-{ac_type}: {duration:.0f} min, {fuel:.0f} kg")
    ax.scatter([coords[a][0] for a in airports], [coords[a][1] for a in airports], marker="^", s=65, c="#33333")
    for airport in airports: ax.annotate(airport, coords[airport], xytext=(4, 4), textcoords="offset points", fontsize=9)
    ax.set_title("典型可行架次航线（燃油约束校验通过）"); ax.legend(fontsize=8); ax.set_aspect("equal")
    return _save(fig, "routes", prefix)

    metrics = _fact_metrics()
    names = ["机时", "在途时间", "燃油", "座位利用率"]
    keys = ["duration", "travel", "fuel", "utilization"]
```

```python
fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12 / 2.54, 7.2 / 2.54))
bars = ax.bar(["V2基线", "临时任务修复", "顺序压缩", "V3最终"], values, bottom=bottoms, color=colors)
ax.axhline(0, color="#444444", linewidth=0.8)
ax.set_ylim(-35, 68)
ax.set_ylabel("相对 V2 基线的变化 (min)"); ax.set_title("Q3 Stage-B 改进链")
for bar, value in zip(bars[1:], values[1]:]):
    y = bar.get_y() + bar.get_height()
    ax.annotate(f"{value:+.0f}", (bar.get_x() + bar.get_width() / 2, y), xytext=(0, 5 if value >= 0 else -7), textcoords="offset points", ha="center", va="bottom" if value >= 0 else "top", fontsize=8)
    ax.text(0.98, 0.96, f"V3={final:.0f} min\n临时任务 158→160 人",
transform=ax.transAxes, ha="right", va="top", fontsize=8)
    return _save(fig, "q3_waterfall", prefix)

def fig_sortie_mix(prefix=""):
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(14 / 2.54, 5.8 / 2.54), sharey=True)
    for ax, q in zip(axes, (1, 2, 3)):
    groups = _group_sorties(_routes(q))
    counts = Counter(key[0] for key in groups)
    types = ["T1", "T2", "T3"]
    bars = ax.bar(types, [counts[t] for t in types], color=CB_COLORS[:3])
    ax.set_title(f"Q{q}"); ax.bar_label(bars, fontsize=8)
    axes[0].set_ylabel("架次数"); fig.suptitle("三向机型架次构成")
    return _save(fig, "sortie_mix", prefix)

def fig_q3_calendar(prefix=" ):
    rows = _routes(3)
    counts = Counter()
    for row in rows:
    if row["stop_order"] == "0" and row.get("departure_time"):
    date = row["departure_time"].split()[0]
    airport = row["aircraft_id"].split("-")[0]
    counts[(date, airport)] += 1
    dates = sorted({date for date, _in counts})
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(14 / 2.54, 6.2 / 2.54))
    x = np.arange(len(dates)); bottom = np.zeros(len(dates))
    for color, airport in zip(CB_COLORS[:3], ("A01", "A02", "A03")),
    values = [counts[(date, airport)] for date in dates]
    ax.bar(x, values, bottom=bottom, label=airport, color=color); bottom += values
    ax.set_xticks(x, dates, rotation=25); ax.set_ylabel("架次数");
    ax.set_title("Q3 每日机场架次排班")
    ax.legend(fontsize=8)
    return _save(fig, "q3_calendar", prefix)

FIGURES = {
    "network": fig_network,
    "heatmap": fig_heatmap,
    "tasks": fig_tasks,
    "routes": fig_routes,
    "v3_compare": fig_v3_compare,
    "ablation": fig_ablation,
    "optimality_gap": fig_optimality_gap,
    "q3_waterfall": fig_q3_waterfall,
    "sortie_mix": fig_sortie_mix,
    "q3_calendar": fig_q3_calendar,
```

```python
def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="B题论文图表生成器")
    parser.add_argument("--fig", default="all", help="逗号分隔图名；默认生成全部")
    parser.add_argument("--test", action="store_true", help="输出_test_前缀测试图")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    prefix = "_test_" if args.test else ""
    wanted = list(FIGURES) if args.fig == "all" else [name.strip() for name in args.fig.split(",")] _load()
    for name in wanted:
    if name not in FIGURES:
    raise SystemExit(f"未知图表：{name}; 可选：{'，'.join(FIGURES)}")
    output = FIGURES[name](prefix)
    print(f"OK {output.name} -> {output}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## A.3 复现命令与结果对应

复现步骤：将官方四份数据文件保持原文件名放入source/problem目录，安装coptpy与常规科学计算依赖后，在source目录下执行reproduce.ps1（或逐条执行下列命令）。

```powershell
$py = '<Python 3.11 解释器>'
Set-Location '<解压后的项目目录>/source'
& $py code\pipeline_v4.py --q 1 --timeout 600 --pricing-rounds 3 --pricing-seconds 60 --out code\results\experiments\v4-final-q1
& $py code\pipeline_v4.py --q 2 --timeout 900 --pricing-rounds 3 --pricing-seconds 60 --out code\results\experiments\v4-final-q2
& $py code\pipeline_v3.py --q 3 --timeout 600 --out code\results\experiments\v3-final-q3
& $py code\strict_validator.py --q 1 --out code\results\experiments\v4-final-q1
& $py code\strict_validator.py --q 2 --out code\results\experiments\v4-final-q2
& $py code\strict_validator.py --q 3 --out code\results\experiments\v3-final-q3
```

预期校验输出与论文结果对应关系如下：

```txt
STRICT_Q1 PASS routes=87 assigned=1600/1600 → 论文第六章：总飞机使用时间15414分钟，人员总在途时间149683分钟
STRICT_Q2 PASS routes=226 assigned=4000/4000 → 论文第七章：总飞机使用时间39646分钟，人员总在途时间283608分钟
STRICT_Q3 PASS routes=594 assigned=4000/4000 → 论文第八章：总飞机使用时间100509分钟，临时任务160/160全覆盖
```

支撑材料results目录保存了与上述命令对应的正式输出：v4-final-q1、v4-final-q2各含航线文件、指派文件、V4报告与运行日志，为Q1/Q2正式提交结果；v3-final-q3含Q3正式结果；v3-ablation为消融实验数据；v3-q3-stageb-apply-all为Q3 Stage-B对照结果；v3-final-q1/q2与v2目录保留同口径历史结果供版本对比。validation/strict-validation.txt记录三问独立严格校验的完整通过日志。

## A.4 AI工具使用声明与使用详情

工具使用声明（与正文参考文献前所设声明一致）：本参赛队在竞赛过程中使用了AI工具，主要用于文字润色、代码调试、格式检查和运行日志整理，详细使用情况见支撑材料。

AI工具使用详情以下内容与支撑材料中“AI工具使用详情.pdf”完全一致。

## A.4.1 一、使用工具

本项目使用OpenAI Codex桌面端及其GPT-5.6-sol模型进行辅助。

A.4.2 二、具体使用目的和环节AI工具主要用于辅助性工程环节，包括官方格式条款整理、文档结构检查、文字表述修订、文件清单生成、命令编排、测试执行和运行日志归纳。开发过程中也使用AI辅助定位局部程序错误，并提出少量补丁实现建议，例如路线索引、时间窗插入和结果校验接口的检查。

赛题约束解释、决策变量和目标函数选择、核心数学模型与算法架构、实验口径、COPT模型含义、结果有效性判断和最终论文结论由参赛队自主完成、确定。AI输出不作为模型正确性或结果合规性的直接依据。

## A.4.3 三、主要提示方式和使用过程

典型提示包括：要求依据原题逐项检查路线、座位、燃油、时间窗和机队约束；要求比较程序输出与独立严格校验结果；要求根据官方论文格式整理匿名提交材料；要求检查局部代码是否存在索引、容量或时序错误。AI返回的内容主要是检查清单、可能的错误位置、补丁建议和命令建议。

未向AI提供参赛者姓名、学校、联系方式、账号密钥或其他身份信息。支撑材料中也不包含上述信息。

## A.4.4 四、采纳、人工修改和核验

所有AI建议均由参赛队逐项检查后决定是否采纳。代码改动经过人工阅读，并使用固定Python环境实际运行；关键结果经过Python编译检查、回归测试、COPT求解日志和独立严格校验器复核。Q1、Q2、Q3的正式结果分别通过STRICT\_Q1 PASS、STRICT\_Q2PASS和STRICT\_Q3PASS。AI产生的文字没有直接作为最终论文内容提交，需由参赛队结合原题、程序和结果文件重新核对、修改。对于不符合原题、无法复现或没有验证证据的建议均不采纳。

## A.4.5 五、责任边界

参赛队对数学模型、算法架构、源程序、实验设计、结果解释和最终提交材料承担全部责任。 工具仅提供辅助，不替代参赛队对核心建模、核心算法和提交内容的人工判断与核验。
